```python
        result, result_bytes = loader.read_manifest(result_path, "M5 RESULT")
        _require_pretty_bytes(run, run_bytes, "M5 RUN")
        _require_pretty_bytes(result, result_bytes, "M5 RESULT")
        schemas = _pinned_schema_map(run, loader)
        _validate_run_semantics(run, schemas["M5_RUN"], loader)
        _validate_result_semantics(
            run,
            result,
            schemas["M5_RESULT"],
            loader,
            expected_public_key_sha256=expected_authority_sha256,
        )
        _validate_done_semantics(
            run, result, done, run_bytes, result_bytes, schemas["M5_DONE"]
        )
        expected = [
            immutable.ImmutableFile(
                done_relative,
                immutable.sha256_bytes(done_bytes),
                len(done_bytes),
                source_path=root / done_relative,
            ),
            immutable.ImmutableFile(
                run_path,
                run_pin["sha256"],
                run_pin["size_bytes"],
                source_path=root / run_path,
            ),
            immutable.ImmutableFile(
                result_path,
                result_pin["sha256"],
                result_pin["size_bytes"],
                source_path=root / result_path,
            ),
        ]
        expected.extend(
            immutable.ImmutableFile(
                row["relative_path"],
                row["sha256"],
                row["size_bytes"],
                source_path=root / row["relative_path"],
            )
            for row in done["artifact_inventory"]
        )
        try:
            immutable.verify_published_files(root, expected)
        except immutable.ImmutableArtifactError as exc:
            _fail(f"relocated bundle byte verification failed: {exc}")
        loader.verify_root()
        return done
    finally:
        loader.close()


@_contract_boundary
def collect(*_arguments: Any, **_keywords: Any) -> None:
    _fail(M5_COLLECT_DISABLED)


def _parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    subcommands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    plan_parser = subcommands.add_parser("plan")
    plan_parser.add_argument("--request", "--input", dest="request", type=Path, required=True)
    plan_parser.add_argument("--output-root", "--output", dest="output_root", type=Path, required=True)
    analyze_parser = subcommands.add_parser("analyze")
    analyze_parser.add_argument("--run", type=Path, required=True)
    analyze_parser.add_argument("--request", "--input", dest="request", type=Path, required=True)
    analyze_parser.add_argument("--output-root", "--output", dest="output_root", type=Path, required=True)
    verify_parser = subcommands.add_parser("verify")
    verify_parser.add_argument("bundle_positional", nargs="?", type=Path)
    verify_parser.add_argument("--bundle", dest="bundle_option", type=Path)
    verify_parser.add_argument("--expected-authority-sha256", required=True)
    collect_parser = subcommands.add_parser("collect")
    collect_parser.add_argument("arguments", nargs=argparse.REMAINDER)
    return parser


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    arguments = list(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)
    if arguments and arguments[0] == "collect":
        sys.stderr.write(f"{M5_COLLECT_DISABLED}\n")
        return 2
    parser = _parser()
    args = parser.parse_args(arguments)
    try:
        if args.command == "plan":
            print(plan(args.request, args.output_root))
            return 0
        if args.command == "analyze":
            print(analyze(args.run, args.request, args.output_root))
            return 0
        if args.command == "verify":
            bundle = args.bundle_option or args.bundle_positional
            if bundle is None or (
                args.bundle_option is not None and args.bundle_positional is not None
            ):
                parser.error("verify requires exactly one bundle path")
            done = verify(bundle, args.expected_authority_sha256)
            print(f"M5_VERIFIED {done['model_seal_sha256']}")
            return 0
        _fail(f"unsupported command: {args.command}")
    except (M5ContractError, immutable.ImmutableArtifactError) as exc:
        sys.stderr.write(f"M5_CONTRACT_ERROR: {exc}\n")
        return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import hashlib
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Mapping

import pytest
from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator

from tools.local_labels import immutable_artifact as immutable
from tools.local_labels import m5_calibration_controller as m5


_COMMIT = "1" * 40
_SEED = bytes(range(32))


def _encode_point(point: tuple[int, int, int, int]) -> bytes:
    x, y, z, _t = point
    inverse = pow(z, m5._ED25519_Q - 2, m5._ED25519_Q)
    x = x * inverse % m5._ED25519_Q
    y = y * inverse % m5._ED25519_Q
    encoded = bytearray(y.to_bytes(32, "little"))
    encoded[31] |= (x & 1) << 7
    return bytes(encoded)


def _test_key() -> tuple[bytes, int, bytes]:
    digest = hashlib.sha512(_SEED).digest()
    scalar = int.from_bytes(digest[:32], "little")
    scalar &= (1 << 254) - 8
    scalar |= 1 << 254
    public = _encode_point(m5._ed25519_scalar_mult(m5._ED25519_BASE, scalar))
    return public, scalar, digest[32:]


def _sign(message: bytes) -> bytes:
    public, scalar, prefix = _test_key()
    nonce = int.from_bytes(hashlib.sha512(prefix + message).digest(), "little") % m5._ED25519_L
    encoded_r = _encode_point(m5._ed25519_scalar_mult(m5._ED25519_BASE, nonce))
    challenge = int.from_bytes(
        hashlib.sha512(encoded_r + public + message).digest(), "little"
    ) % m5._ED25519_L
    signature_scalar = (nonce + challenge * scalar) % m5._ED25519_L
    return encoded_r + signature_scalar.to_bytes(32, "little")


def _write(path: Path, payload: bytes) -> Path:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_bytes(payload)
    return path


def _write_json(path: Path, value: Any) -> Path:
    return _write(path, immutable.pretty_json_bytes(value))


def _input(artifact_id: str, path: Path, media_type: str = "application/json") -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"artifact_id": artifact_id, "path": str(path), "media_type": media_type}


def _identity(root: Path, identity_id: str, kind: str, version: str, payload: bytes, media_type: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    path = _write(root / "identity-inputs" / identity_id, payload)
    return {
        "identity_id": identity_id,
        "identity_kind": kind,
        "version": version,
        "artifact_input": _input(f"{identity_id}-input", path, media_type),
    }


def _plan_request(root: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    public, _scalar, _prefix = _test_key()
    identities = [
        _identity(root, "m5-analysis-authority", "AUTHORITY", "ed25519-v1", public, "application/vnd.m5.ed25519-public-key"),
        _identity(root, "source-main", "SOURCE", "v1", b"source\n", "text/x-cuda"),
        _identity(root, "harness-main", "HARNESS", "v1", b"harness\n", "text/x-python"),
        _identity(root, "build-main", "BUILD", "v1", b"build\n", "application/json"),
        _identity(root, "binary-main", "BINARY", "v1", b"binary\n", "application/octet-stream"),
    ]
    relationship = {
        "relationship_id": "build-rel-main",
        "source_identity_ids": ["source-main", "m5-pchase-source"],
        "build_identity_id": "build-main",
        "binary_identity_id": "binary-main",
    }
    rank = {family: index + 1 for index, family in enumerate(m5.SCIENTIFIC_RANK)}
    families = [
        {
            "family_id": family,
            "execution_index": index,
            "scientific_rank": rank[family],
            "harness_identity_ids": ["harness-main"],
            "build_relationship_ids": ["build-rel-main"],
        }
        for index, family in enumerate(m5.EXECUTION_ORDER, start=1)
    ]
    quantities = copy.deepcopy(m5._quantity_authority_model()["quantities"])
    pchase_ids = [q["quantity_id"] for q in quantities if q["producer"]["kind"] == "PCHASE"]
    return {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "request_kind": "M5_PLAN_REQUEST",
        "expected_source_commit": _COMMIT,
        "target": {"architecture": "sm_86", "chip": "GA102", "sku": "RTX 3090 Ti", "device_uuid_sha256": "2" * 64},
        "operating_state": {
            "state_id": "locked-sm86", "sm_clock_mhz": 1350, "memory_clock_mhz": 10501,
            "persistence_mode": True, "clock_locked": True, "cache_control": "all", "throttle_state": "idle",
            "driver_version": "610.43.02", "vbios_version": "test-vbios",
            "core_memory_relationship": {"kind": "LOCKED_FIXED_PAIR", "core_mhz": 1350, "memory_mhz": 10501, "reduced_core": 1350, "reduced_memory": 10501},
        },
        "pins": [{"pin_id": "source-authority", "value": _COMMIT, "authority_identity_id": "source-main", "authority_kind": "SOURCE"}],
        "identities": identities,
        "build_relationships": [relationship],
        "family_execution_order": list(m5.EXECUTION_ORDER),
        "family_scientific_rank": list(m5.SCIENTIFIC_RANK),
        "families": families,
        "quantities": quantities,
        "components": {
            "pchase": {"component_id": "pchase", "required": True, "harness_identity_ids": ["harness-main"], "build_relationship_ids": ["build-rel-main"], "quantity_ids": pchase_ids},
            "roster_control_sidecars": {
                "component_id": "roster_control_sidecars", "required": True, "expected_inventory_id": "control-inventory", "expected_binary_count": 1,
                "members": [{"binary_id": "binary-main", "binary_identity_id": "binary-main", "expected_sidecar_id": "control.calibration-main", "witness_slug": "calibration_main", "shape_point": "m5_locked"}],
            },
        },
        "policy": {
            "ncu_cross_check_only": True, "ncu_derivation_forbidden": True, "ncu_selection_forbidden": True,
            "ncu_tuning_forbidden": True, "ncu_transcription_forbidden": True, "simulator_fit_forbidden": True,
            "architecture_card_write_forbidden": True, "publication_mode": "ADDITIVE_EVIDENCE_ONLY",
        },
    }


def _control_record() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "pc": 0, "predicate": None, "opcode_text": "MOV R0, R0", "low_word": "0x0000000000000000", "high_word": "0x0000000000000000",
        "control": {"stall": 0, "yield_flag": 0, "write_barrier": 0, "read_barrier": 0, "wait_mask": 0, "reuse": 0}, "status": "decoded",
    }


def _sidecar(function_name: str, binary_name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "arch": "sm_86", "functions": {function_name: [_control_record()]}, "skipped_arch_sections": [],
        "parser_contract": copy.deepcopy(m5.CONTROL_PARSER_CONTRACT), "toolkit_version": "V13.2.0", "binary": binary_name,
        "summary": {"functions": {function_name: {"instructions": 1, "decoded": 1, "unknown_encoding": 0, "stall_cycles_static_sum": 0}}, "total_decoded": 1, "total_unknown_encoding": 0},
    }


def _full_join_cell(member: Mapping[str, Any], function_name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "identity": {"slug": member["slug"], "shape_point": member["shape_point"], "binary_sha256": member["binary_sha256"]},
        "first_launch": "kernel_1", "vpc_low32_mismatches": 0, "n_instruction_records": 1,
        "functions": [{
            "function_unique_id": 1, "kernel_name": f"{function_name}___0", "sass_function": function_name, "status": "joined",
            "n_traced_pcs": 1, "n_joined": 1, "n_decoded": 1, "missing_pcs": [],
            "sample": {"pc": 0, "opcode_text": "MOV R0, R0", "control": _control_record()["control"]},
        }],
        "coverage": {"n_traced_pcs": 1, "n_joined": 1, "n_decoded": 1, "full_join": True},
    }


def _typed_join_cell(member: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "identity": {"slug": member["slug"], "shape_point": member["shape_point"], "binary_sha256": member["binary_sha256"]},
        "status": "typed", "typed_reason": "binary_or_trace_unresolved",
    }


class CampaignBuilder:
    def __init__(self, root: Path, run: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
        self.root = root
        self.run = run
        self.counter = 0
        self.repetition_counter = 0

    def authenticated(self, artifact_id: str, payload: bytes, role: str, context_id: str, media_type: str = "application/json") -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any], Path]:
        self.counter += 1
        artifact_path = _write(self.root / "analysis-inputs" / f"artifact-{self.counter:04d}", payload)
        digest = immutable.sha256_bytes(payload)
        reference = m5._artifact_reference(artifact_id, digest, len(payload), media_type)
        unsigned = {
            "schema_version": 1, "artifact_kind": "M5_AUTHORITY_RECEIPT", "authority_identity_id": "m5-analysis-authority",
            "run_model_seal_sha256": self.run["model_seal_sha256"], "source_commit": self.run["source_state"]["source_commit"],
            "artifact_id": artifact_id, "artifact_sha256": digest, "artifact_size_bytes": len(payload), "artifact_media_type": media_type,
            "artifact_role": role, "context_id": context_id,
        }
        signature = _sign(m5._RECEIPT_DOMAIN + immutable.canonical_json_bytes(unsigned))
        receipt = {**unsigned, "signature_ed25519": signature.hex()}
        receipt_id = f"receipt.{self.counter:04d}.{hashlib.sha256(context_id.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]}"
        receipt_path = _write_json(self.root / "analysis-inputs" / f"receipt-{self.counter:04d}.json", receipt)
        return {
            "artifact_input": _input(artifact_id, artifact_path, media_type),
            "receipt_input": _input(receipt_id, receipt_path),
        }, reference, artifact_path

    def repetitions(self) -> list[str]:
        result = []
        for _ in range(3):
            self.repetition_counter += 1
            result.append(f"rep-{self.repetition_counter:04d}")
        return result

    def build(self) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Path]]:
        quantity_results = []
        repetition_ids: list[str] = []
        producer_paths: dict[str, Path] = {}
        for quantity in self.run["quantities"]:
            quantity_id = quantity["quantity_id"]
            evidence: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
            evidence_ids: dict[str, str] = {}
            for role in m5.EVIDENCE_ROLES:
                artifact_id = f"evidence.{quantity_id}.{role}"
                evidence_ids[role] = artifact_id
                payload = immutable.pretty_json_bytes({"artifact_kind": "M5_TEST_EVIDENCE", "quantity_id": quantity_id, "role": role})
                auth, _reference, _path = self.authenticated(artifact_id, payload, f"EVIDENCE_{role.upper()}", f"{quantity_id}.{role}.0")
                evidence[role] = [auth]
            readout_inputs = []
            producer_hashes = []
            for contract in quantity["readout_contracts"]:
                repetitions = self.repetitions()
                repetition_ids.extend(repetitions)
                probability = quantity["value_domain"]["kind"] == "PROBABILITY"
                observations = [
                    {
                        "repetition_id": repetition,
                        "launch_id": f"launch-{repetition}",
                        "numerator": 1 if probability else 10,
                        "denominator": 2 if probability else 1,
                    }
                    for repetition in repetitions
                ]
                lineage_id = f"lineage.{contract['readout_id']}"
                raw_sample = {
                    "schema_version": 1,
                    "artifact_kind": "M5_RAW_SAMPLE_SERIES",
                    "run_model_seal_sha256": self.run["model_seal_sha256"],
                    "quantity_id": quantity_id,
                    "readout_id": contract["readout_id"],
                    "method_class": contract["method_class"],
                    "instrument": contract["instrument"],
                    "source_lineage_id": lineage_id,
                    "harness_identity_id": "harness-main",
                    "build_relationship_id": "build-rel-main",
                    "binary_identity_id": "binary-main",
                    "observations": observations,
                    "ncu_ancestor_artifact_ids": [],
                    "simulator_ancestor_artifact_ids": [],
                }
                raw_id = f"raw.{contract['readout_id']}"
                raw_auth, raw_reference, raw_path = self.authenticated(
                    raw_id,
                    immutable.pretty_json_bytes(raw_sample),
                    "RAW_SAMPLE_SERIES",
                    contract["readout_id"],
                )
                producer = {
                    "schema_version": 1, "artifact_kind": "M5_READOUT_PRODUCER", "run_model_seal_sha256": self.run["model_seal_sha256"],
                    "quantity_id": quantity_id, "readout_id": contract["readout_id"], "method_class": contract["method_class"],
                    "instrument": contract["instrument"], "derivation_identity_id": contract["derivation_identity_id"],
                    "derivation_operation": contract["derivation_operation"], "unit": quantity["unit"], "input_class": contract["instrument"],
                    "sample_operation": contract["sample_operation"], "raw_sample_artifact_sha256": raw_reference["sha256"],
                    "source_lineage_id": lineage_id,
                    "evidence_artifact_ids": [evidence_ids[role] for role in contract["required_evidence_roles"]],
                    "ncu_artifact_ids": [], "simulator_artifact_ids": [],
                }
                payload = immutable.pretty_json_bytes(producer)
                producer_id = f"producer.{contract['readout_id']}"
                producer_auth, reference, path = self.authenticated(producer_id, payload, "READOUT_PRODUCER", contract["readout_id"])
                producer_paths[contract["readout_id"]] = path
                producer_paths[f"raw.{contract['readout_id']}"] = raw_path
                readout_inputs.append({"raw_sample": raw_auth, "producer": producer_auth})
                producer_hashes.append(reference["sha256"])
            decision = {
                "schema_version": 1, "artifact_kind": "M5_QUANTITY_DECISION", "run_model_seal_sha256": self.run["model_seal_sha256"],
                "quantity_id": quantity_id, "readout_producer_sha256s": producer_hashes, "outcome": {"kind": "MEASURED"},
                "ncu_artifact_ids": [], "simulator_artifact_ids": [],
            }
            decision_auth, _ref, _path = self.authenticated(f"decision.{quantity_id}", immutable.pretty_json_bytes(decision), "QUANTITY_DECISION", quantity_id)
            quantity_results.append({"quantity_id": quantity_id, "evidence": evidence, "readout_inputs": readout_inputs, "decision": decision_auth, "cross_checks": []})

        def state(observation_id: str, phase: str, repetition_id: str | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
            return {
                "observation_id": observation_id, "observation_phase": phase, "repetition_id": repetition_id,
                "sm_clock_mhz": 1350, "memory_clock_mhz": 10501, "persistence_mode": True, "clock_locked": True,
                "cache_control": "all", "driver_version": "610.43.02", "throttle_reasons": [],
            }

        protocol = {
            "schema_version": 1, "artifact_kind": "M5_PROTOCOL_STATE_WITNESS", "run_model_seal_sha256": self.run["model_seal_sha256"],
            "source_commit": self.run["source_state"]["source_commit"], "protocol_identity_id": "m5-campaign-protocol",
            "m0": state("obs-m0", "M0", None), "global_pre": state("obs-global-pre", "PRE", None), "global_post": state("obs-global-post", "POST", None),
            "repetitions": [{"repetition_id": repetition, "pre": state(f"obs-pre-{repetition}", "PRE", repetition), "post": state(f"obs-post-{repetition}", "POST", repetition)} for repetition in repetition_ids],
        }
        protocol_auth, _ref, protocol_path = self.authenticated("m5-protocol-state-witness", immutable.pretty_json_bytes(protocol), "PROTOCOL_STATE_WITNESS", "protocol-state")

        def control_group(members: list[Mapping[str, Any]], group_id: str, witness_id: str, complete_first_only: bool) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], Path]:
            cells = []
            entries = []
            full_count = 0
            for index, member in enumerate(members):
                function_name = f"function_{group_id.lower()}_{index}"
                sidecar_auth, _reference, _path = self.authenticated(
                    member["expected_sidecar_id"], immutable.pretty_json_bytes(_sidecar(function_name, member["cell_id"])), "CONTROL_SIDECAR", member["cell_id"]
                )
                entries.append({"cell_id": member["cell_id"], "expected_sidecar_id": member["expected_sidecar_id"], "sidecar": sidecar_auth})
                complete = index == 0 if complete_first_only else True
                if complete:
                    cells.append(_full_join_cell(member, function_name))
                    full_count += 1
                else:
                    cells.append(_typed_join_cell(member))
            witness = {
                "schema_version": 1, "artifact_kind": "sass_control_pc_join_witness", "arch": "sm_86",
                "source_commit": self.run["source_state"]["source_commit"], "n_cells": len(cells), "n_full_join": full_count, "cells": cells,
            }
            witness_auth, _reference, path = self.authenticated(witness_id, immutable.pretty_json_bytes(witness), "CONTROL_PC_JOIN_WITNESS", group_id)
            return {"pc_join_witness": witness_auth, "entries": entries}, path

        canonical_members = self.run["components"]["canonical_runnable_roster"]["members"]
        calibration_members = self.run["components"]["roster_control_sidecars"]["calibration_members"]
        canonical_controls, canonical_witness_path = control_group(canonical_members, "CANONICAL_ROSTER", "canonical-pc-join-witness", True)
        calibration_controls, calibration_witness_path = control_group(calibration_members, "CALIBRATION_BINARIES", "calibration-pc-join-witness", False)
        request = {
            "schema_version": 1, "request_kind": "M5_ANALYSIS_REQUEST", "run_model_seal_sha256": self.run["model_seal_sha256"],
            "protocol_state_witness": protocol_auth, "quantity_results": quantity_results,
            "roster_control_sidecars": {"canonical": canonical_controls, "calibration": calibration_controls},
        }
        return request, {"protocol": protocol_path, "canonical_witness": canonical_witness_path, "calibration_witness": calibration_witness_path, **producer_paths}


@pytest.fixture(scope="module", autouse=True)
def _stable_source() -> None:
    monkeypatch = pytest.MonkeyPatch()
    monkeypatch.setattr(m5, "_observe_git_source", lambda: (_COMMIT, b""))
    yield
    monkeypatch.undo()


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def campaign(tmp_path_factory: pytest.TempPathFactory) -> dict[str, Any]:
    root = tmp_path_factory.mktemp("m5-campaign")
    request_path = _write_json(root / "plan-request.json", _plan_request(root))
    run_bundle = m5.plan(request_path, root / "run-publications")
    run = json.loads((run_bundle / "RUN.json").read_text())
    builder = CampaignBuilder(root, run)
    analysis_request, input_paths = builder.build()
    analysis_path = _write_json(root / "analysis-request.json", analysis_request)
    final_bundle = m5.analyze(run_bundle, analysis_path, root / "result-publications")
    public, _scalar, _prefix = _test_key()
    return {
        "root": root, "run_bundle": run_bundle, "run": run, "analysis_path": analysis_path,
        "analysis_request": analysis_request, "input_paths": input_paths, "bundle": final_bundle,
        "authority_sha256": immutable.sha256_bytes(public),
    }


def _bundle_models(campaign: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:
    bundle = campaign["bundle"]
    values = [json.loads((bundle / name).read_text()) for name in ("RUN.json", "RESULT.json", "DONE.json")]
    return values[0], values[1], values[2]


def test_plan_analyze_relocate_and_verify(campaign: Mapping[str, Any], tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    run, result, done = _bundle_models(campaign)
    assert run["family_execution_order"] == ["b", "a", "d", "f", "c", "e"]
    assert run["family_scientific_rank"] == ["b", "f", "a", "d", "c", "e"]
    assert run["counts"]["quantity_count"] == 31
    assert run["counts"]["canonical_runnable_cell_count"] == 122
    assert result["counts"]["measured_quantity_count"] == 31
    assert result["counts"]["canonical_control_sidecar_count"] == 122
    assert result["components"]["pchase"]["property_count"] == 14
    assert done["counts"]["authority_receipt_count"] == len(result["authenticity"]["entries"])
    relocated = tmp_path / "relocated"
    shutil.copytree(campaign["bundle"], relocated)
    verified = m5.verify(relocated, campaign["authority_sha256"])
    assert verified["model_seal_sha256"] == done["model_seal_sha256"]


def test_verify_requires_out_of_band_authority(campaign: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(m5.M5ContractError, match="out-of-band trust anchor"):
        m5.verify(campaign["bundle"], "0" * 64)


def test_closed_roster_normalizes_uppercase_without_alias(campaign: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    members = campaign["run"]["components"]["canonical_runnable_roster"]["members"]
    assert len(members) == 122
    assert len({member["cell_id"] for member in members}) == 122
    assert all(m5._SAFE_ID.fullmatch(member["cell_id"]) for member in members)
    uppercase = [member for member in members if any(character.isupper() for character in member["slug"])]
    assert uppercase
    assert all(member["cell_id"] == member["cell_id"].lower() for member in uppercase)


def test_result_rederives_readouts_not_caller_values(campaign: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    run, result, _done = _bundle_models(campaign)
    quantity = run["quantities"][0]
    readout = copy.deepcopy(result["quantity_results"][0]["readouts"][0])
    readout["value"] += 1
    loader = m5._DeclaredSnapshotLoader(root=campaign["bundle"])
    try:
        with pytest.raises(m5.M5ContractError, match="not rederived"):
            m5._rederive_readout(run, quantity, quantity["readout_contracts"][0], readout, loader)
    finally:
        loader.close()


def test_raw_sample_repetition_cardinality_is_exact(campaign: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    run, result, _done = _bundle_models(campaign)
    quantity = run["quantities"][0]
    readout = result["quantity_results"][0]["readouts"][0]
    raw_sample = json.loads(
        (campaign["bundle"] / readout["raw_sample_artifact"]["relative_path"]).read_text()
    )
    raw_sample["observations"][1]["repetition_id"] = raw_sample["observations"][0][
        "repetition_id"
    ]
    modified = immutable.pretty_json_bytes(raw_sample)

    def loader(reference: Mapping[str, Any]) -> bytes:
        if reference["artifact_id"] == readout["raw_sample_artifact"]["artifact_id"]:
            return modified
        return immutable.read_relative(campaign["bundle"], reference["relative_path"])

    with pytest.raises(m5.M5ContractError, match="duplicates a repetition"):
        m5._rederive_readout(run, quantity, quantity["readout_contracts"][0], readout, loader)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("quantity_id", "value", "match"),
    [
        ("pchase.l1_capacity_bytes", -1, "positive"),
        ("pchase.l1_capacity_bytes", 1.5, "integral"),
        ("a.l2_pair_fill_probability", 1.1, r"\[0,1\]"),
        ("e.l2_slice_hash_structure", -0.1, "nonnegative"),
    ],
)
def test_quantity_domains_are_closed(quantity_id: str, value: float, match: str) -> None:
    quantity = next(row for row in m5._quantity_authority_model()["quantities"] if row["quantity_id"] == quantity_id)
    with pytest.raises(m5.M5ContractError, match=match):
        m5._quantity_number(quantity, value, quantity_id)


def test_protocol_covers_every_readout_repetition(campaign: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    _run, result, _done = _bundle_models(campaign)
    expected = sum(readout["sample_count"] for row in result["quantity_results"] for readout in row["readouts"])
    protocol = result["components"]["protocol_state"]
    assert protocol["repetition_count"] == expected == 186
    assert protocol["all_state_joins_passed"] is True


def test_control_inputs_are_real_standard_sidecars_and_pc_witnesses(campaign: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    _run, result, _done = _bundle_models(campaign)
    controls = result["components"]["roster_control_sidecars"]
    assert controls["observed_canonical_cell_count"] == 122
    assert controls["observed_calibration_binary_count"] == 1
    assert controls["entries"][0]["status"] == "COMPLETE"
    assert any(entry["status"] == "TYPED" for entry in controls["entries"][1:122])
    sidecar = json.loads((campaign["bundle"] / controls["entries"][0]["sidecar"]["relative_path"]).read_text())
    assert set(sidecar) == {"arch", "functions", "skipped_arch_sections", "parser_contract", "toolkit_version", "binary", "summary"}


def test_done_inventory_contains_receipts_producers_and_decisions(campaign: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    _run, result, done = _bundle_models(campaign)
    bindings = {binding for row in done["artifact_inventory"] for binding in row["bindings"]}
    assert {"AUTHORITY_RECEIPT", "READOUT_PRODUCER", "QUANTITY_DECISION", "PCHASE_FRESH_CARD"}.issubset(bindings)
    receipt_ids = {row["receipt"]["artifact_id"] for row in result["authenticity"]["entries"]}
    inventory_ids = {row["artifact_id"] for row in done["artifact_inventory"]}
    assert receipt_ids.issubset(inventory_ids)


def test_artifact_relative_path_is_canonical() -> None:
    reference = m5._artifact_reference("artifact", "a" * 64, 1, "text/plain")
    reference["relative_path"] = "artifacts/bad/artifact"
    with pytest.raises(m5.M5ContractError, match="canonical content-addressed"):
        m5._collect_artifact({}, reference, "artifact")


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("payload", "match"),
    [
        (b'{"x":1,"x":2}', "duplicate"),
        (b'{"x":NaN}', "non-finite"),
        (b'{"x":9007199254740992}', "safe range"),
        (b'{"x":9007199254740993.0}', "float exceeds"),
        (b'{"x":"\\ud800"}', "surrogate"),
    ],
)
def test_strict_json_rejects_ambiguous_values(payload: bytes, match: str) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(immutable.ImmutableArtifactError, match=match):
        immutable.strict_json_loads(payload)


def test_strict_json_types_deep_nesting() -> None:
    payload = ("[" * 3000 + "0" + "]" * 3000).encode()
    with pytest.raises(immutable.ImmutableArtifactError, match="invalid|nesting"):
        immutable.strict_json_loads(payload)


def test_invalid_ed25519_authority_is_rejected(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    request = _plan_request(tmp_path)
    authority = next(identity for identity in request["identities"] if identity["identity_id"] == "m5-analysis-authority")
    Path(authority["artifact_input"]["path"]).write_bytes(bytes(32))
    path = _write_json(tmp_path / "request.json", request)
    with pytest.raises(m5.M5ContractError, match="prime-order"):
        m5.plan(path, tmp_path / "out")


def test_direct_collect_refuses_before_optional_imports(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    script = Path(m5.__file__).resolve()
    completed = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-S", str(script), "collect", "--output", str(tmp_path / "never")],
        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30,
    )
    assert completed.returncode == 2
    assert completed.stderr == "M5_COLLECT_DISABLED\n"
    assert not (tmp_path / "never").exists()


def test_collect_function_refuses_without_side_effect(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    marker = tmp_path / "marker"
    with pytest.raises(m5.M5ContractError, match="M5_COLLECT_DISABLED"):
        m5.collect(marker)
    assert not marker.exists()


def test_schemas_are_closed_draft_2020_12() -> None:
    for kind, path in m5.SCHEMA_PATHS.items():
        schema = json.loads(path.read_text())
        Draft202012Validator.check_schema(schema)
        assert schema["$id"] == m5.SCHEMA_IDS[kind]
        assert schema["additionalProperties"] is False
        assert immutable.sha256_file(path)[0] == m5.SCHEMA_SHA256_V1[kind]


def test_read_regular_rejects_symlink_and_hardlink(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    original = _write(tmp_path / "original", b"bytes")
    symlink = tmp_path / "symlink"
    symlink.symlink_to(original)
    hardlink = tmp_path / "hardlink"
    os.link(original, hardlink)
    with pytest.raises(immutable.ImmutableArtifactError, match="regular file"):
        immutable.read_regular(symlink)
    with pytest.raises(immutable.ImmutableArtifactError, match="hard-linked"):
        immutable.read_regular(hardlink)


def test_publication_is_readonly_and_idempotent(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    payload = b"immutable\n"
    file = immutable.ImmutableFile.from_bytes("nested/value.bin", payload)
    output = tmp_path / "out"
    first = immutable.publish_directory(output, "final", [file])
    second = immutable.publish_directory(output, "final", [file])
    assert first == second
    assert (first / "nested" / "value.bin").read_bytes() == payload
    assert not (first.stat().st_mode & 0o222)
    assert not ((first / "nested" / "value.bin").stat().st_mode & 0o222)


def test_verify_pins_nested_inode_through_final_inventory(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    root = tmp_path / "tree"
    nested = root / "nested"
    nested.mkdir(parents=True)
    target = _write(nested / "value", b"old")
    target.chmod(0o444)
    file = immutable.ImmutableFile("nested/value", immutable.sha256_bytes(b"old"), 3, source_path=target)
    original = immutable._verify_open_file
    replaced = False

    def replace_after_hash(descriptor: int, expected: immutable.ImmutableFile) -> bool:
        nonlocal replaced
        result = original(descriptor, expected)
        if not replaced:
            replaced = True
            target.unlink()
            target.write_bytes(b"old")
        return result

    monkeypatch.setattr(immutable, "_verify_open_file", replace_after_hash)
    with pytest.raises(immutable.ImmutableArtifactError, match="path changed"):
        immutable.verify_published_files(root, [file], require_readonly_directories=False)


def test_relative_delta_rejects_overflow() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(m5.M5ContractError, match="overflowed"):
        m5._relative_delta(1.7e308, -1.7e308)

    help_text = m5._parser().format_help()
    assert all(command in help_text for command in ("plan", "analyze", "verify", "collect"))
```

```markdown
## Acceptance Criteria

The `AC-*` items describe the live-campaign completion contract outside RLCR.
All accuracy/coverage claims are against real-GPU NCU under the pinned protocol
v2.1; NCU values are targets/validation only and never enter model or card
inputs (Rule 1).

- AC-1: **Preflight, provenance foundation, and roster authority are established before any capture.** A preflight emitter records host + toolchain + clock state and gates capture; a machine-checkable roster authority enumerates the universe, the runnable set, and the excluded set. Canonical campaign accounting continues to consume restored historical SOL runnability v1. Any fresh SOL reconstitution, shrink, or runnable-count work described below belongs only to the separate supplemental-v3 cohort and remains live-disabled pending its pinned canary; it does not mutate canonical v1.
  - Positive Tests (expected to PASS):
    - Preflight manifest records git HEAD, `working_tree_clean`, NCU/driver/canonical-workload-nvcc/NVBit versions matching the capture pins (NCU 2026.1.0 / driver 610.43.02 / canonical C1 CUDA 13.3 nvcc / NVBit 1.8), separately verifies supplemental SOL launch-set v3's CUDA 13.2 nvcc pin (without enabling live v3 work), separately verifies the independent CUDA 13.2 nvcc used for NVBit/tracer and static-rebind build provenance, clocks == v2.1 (core 1350 / mem 10501, persistence on), a `LD_LIBRARY_PATH=.toolchain/pb/lib` injection smoke that yields a nonzero contract-valid trace, and declared free disk > the authored trace-retention budget.
    - The roster authority manifest resolves to a total universe count, a runnable-set count, and an excluded-set count with `universe == runnable + excluded`, each row carrying `roster_source`, binary, app-args, shape selector, and sha256 pins.
  - Negative Tests (expected to FAIL):
    - A dirty unrecorded worktree, unlocked host clocks for the reference config, a missing `.toolchain/pb` runtime, or a missing trace-budget file causes preflight to fail BEFORE any capture runs.
    - A roster authority where any runnable cell lacks a resolvable binary/shape/app-args, or where `universe != runnable + excluded`, fails.

- **Supplemental-v3 contract — outside AC-1 and canonical campaign-M4/M7; live-disabled until its pinned canary passes.** It cannot gate, expand, or mutate canonical v1.
  - Positive test after canary authorization: SOL-ExecBench reconstitution reproduces a separate runnable/deferred split on this host (data via `download_data.sh`, environment via `uv sync`, CUTLASS via `CUTLASS_DIR` override) fingerprinted by a native-env hash; each of the 23 historical OOM cells is first re-checked under `--replay-mode application`, then, only if still needed, shrunk per `plans/sm86_sol_shrink_to_fit_fable5_answer.md` with full native-shape and regime provenance.
  - Negative tests after canary authorization: a `shape_adjusted` cell lacking its native-shape reference or typed failure reason fails; a shrink that cuts attention B·H below CTA saturation without a regime flag fails; a cell whose post-capture `dram__bytes.sum` deviates > ±30% from its footprint model without demotion to `reduced_representative` fails.

- AC-2: **The frozen campaign-M1 bundle is a green pre-flip regression anchor.** `eval/frozen_labels/sm86_m1_bundle_v21.json` validates and replays under old-contract semantics, byte-identical, before the flip.
  - Positive Tests: replay/validate the frozen bundle → 15 admitted / 2 typed-excluded cells; `smsp__inst_executed.sum` EXACT 15/15; L1 op_ld sector-hit-rate label-exact; all bundle sha256 pins reproduce.
  - Negative Tests: any new validation failure, changed admitted/excluded count, or sha256 mismatch on the frozen bundle fails (guards against accidental pre-flip drift or a premature contract change).

- AC-3: **The tracer trace-contract transition lands as ONE lockstepped, capability-versioned commit with consumer widening and synthetic tests — semantically correct for atomics vs async-copy, with BOTH flips included.**
  - AC-3.1: The producer flip stops the host-side drop of the addressed classes and stamps a manifest-level `trace_contract_id` plus a producer capability list (`memory_payload_classes`); the SASS classifier is untouched; the proto is regenerated and re-pinned in the same commit.
  - AC-3.2: Consumers are widened in lockstep to ENFORCE identity, not merely decode the enum: `import_trace_contract` extends its global-class allocation-identity gate to address-bearing atomic/async-copy records; `tools/sector_stream/generator.py`, HRD, `order_projections`, and the cache-access policy are aligned to the new payload classes; the Rust `sim/trace` reader enforces the same identity the Python gate does.
  - AC-3.3: Synthetic producer fixtures cover ordinary global load, **global atomic**, **shared atomic (ATOMS)**, **RED**, **LDGSTS (cp.async)**, and predicated-off memory records. The ATOMIC flip was implemented and tested first WITHIN this single contract transition; the ASYNC_COPY (LDGSTS) flip was adjudicated (source/destination operand semantics proven), widened, tested, and included in the SAME contract transition before the transition completed. "Atomic-first" was an internal implementation ordering only — never "atomic-only now, async later"; campaign M2 did not complete and full-roster capture (AC-5) did not start until BOTH flips were in the contract.
  - Positive Tests: a new-contract trace with an active global atomic or LDGSTS-source record carries `allocation_id` + `memory_op_class` + `data_width`; a shared-atomic (ATOMS) record is classified without being forced through the global allocation-id rule; the frozen campaign-M1 traces still validate with the capability absent.
  - Negative Tests: a new-contract active global-atomic / LDGSTS-source record with `allocation_id` absent or zero is REJECTED; forcing a shared atomic through global allocation identity is REJECTED; a `.proto` change committed without its pin regeneration fails `cargo test`.

- AC-4: **A new-contract canary proves the flip unlocked scorability without regressing exact metrics.** A small canary set (ordinary load, global atomic, shared-atomic/RED, LDGSTS) is captured under the new contract.
  - Positive Tests: the canary manifest shows the new `trace_contract_id` + capability list, nonzero admitted atomic/async records where expected, and no unexpected `unsupported_memory_path` rows; a previously-`na` tensor-core op_ld cell (e.g. `gemm_hmma_bf16`) and the atomic histogram L2 cell become scorable; `smsp__inst_executed.sum` and L1 op_ld exactness on the pre-existing cells are unchanged.
  - Negative Tests: a canary cell that still emits address-stripped `unsupported_memory_path` for the flipped classes fails; any regression in `smsp__inst_executed.sum` or L1 op_ld exactness on the pre-existing cells fails.

- AC-5: **The full runnable roster is captured once under the new contract at the v2.1 reference protocol** — traces + full-metric-vector labels + control-bits — with resume/idempotence, per-chunk provenance, and typed sampling for budget-breaching cells.
  - Positive Tests: for every runnable cell, admitted outputs contain a full-registry NCU label sidecar (the sm_86-available metric set), a contract-valid NVBit trace, a control-bits sidecar, a binary-sha256 join, and a v2.1 protocol stamp; the roster-authority count equals admitted + typed-excluded; re-running a completed chunk skips cells whose sidecar hashes already match; each immutable chunk emits a DONE-manifest (command, source_commit, dirty-state, tool versions, protocol, roster hash, budget hash, output root, pass/fail counts, typed-failure rows) and passes a pre/post source-commit equality check; a cell that breaches the trace budget is handled per the AC-9 sampling ladder (compression-first, admissibility-gated, wave-segment/reduced-shape, typed) — never silently truncated.
  - Negative Tests: any runnable cell present in NEITHER admitted outputs NOR the exclusion ledger fails the phase; a cell whose repeats span differing `source_commit` (mid-sweep commit → `label_provenance_duplicate`) fails; a budget-breaching cell captured without the AC-9 sampling ladder and its typed provenance fails; an incomplete trace (block coverage < 100% vs the launch `gridDim`) accepted without a `trace_sampling` sidecar fails.

- AC-6: **Calibration cards are refreshed/extended from the Fable-5-designed six-probe clock64 suite, Rule-1 clean, overdetermined, and stored as additive evidence in the current campaign scope** (designs: `plans/sm86_calibration_microbench_design_fable5_answer.md` for (a)–(e) + `plans/sm86_cp_async_timing_probe_fable5_answer.md` for (f)). pchase is re-run under the lock; the suite is built in **load-bearing order (b) port-width → (f) cp.async/LDGSTS timing → (a) L2↔DRAM fetch-granularity → (d) MSHR/MLP saturation → (c) achievable DRAM bandwidth → (e) L2 associativity/slice-hash**, at **execution order b, a, d, f, c, e** (f consumes (b)'s port + (d)'s MSHR verdicts, so it runs after both); (b)+(d) run as a Little's-law-coupled pair, and (f) = the F-L completion-latency + F-T streaming-plateau + F-B burst-issue sub-probes; control-bits are extended roster-wide. The sim `LDGSTS` event-model that consumes (f)'s constants (f1–f5) and the read-only attribution pass that confirms (f)'s provisional rank are downstream (FUT-6).
  - Positive Tests: each probe emits a measured value OR a typed bounded gap from the taxonomy {`RANGE`, `CENSORED`, `MIXTURE`, `UNRESOLVED_STRUCTURE`, `SURFACE_GAP`} carrying its full evidence curve + in-run positive/negative controls + confound verdicts, written as ADDITIVE evidence files (not transcribed into the byte-pinned `arch_profiles/cards/sm_86.json` in the current campaign scope, per DEC-3); every card constant is **overdetermined by ≥ 2 independent probe readouts that must agree under the hardware hypothesis** (Little's-law triangulation (b)×(d), RTT-contrast, persistence/Δ-contrast); control-bits sidecars exist for every runnable binary (0 unknown_encoding, or typed); a Rule-1 guard proves no NCU metric entered any card-input derivation.
  - Negative Tests: any card input traceable to an NCU metric fails the Rule-1 guard; a microbench that produces neither a value nor a typed bounded gap (a silent guess) fails; a card constant derived from a SINGLE readout or tuned against the simulator's end-to-end output fails the overdetermination rule (cross-probe contradictions are recorded as typed contradictions, never averaged away); an edit to `arch_profiles/cards/sm_86.json` transcribing a microbench value in the current campaign scope fails the DEC-3 boundary check.

- AC-7: **The full cross-protocol repeatability matrix is captured and analyzed by the Fable-5 uncensored determinism-study design** (full design: `plans/sm86_determinism_study_stats_fable5_answer.md`). Cells × {clock none/base/boost} × {cache all/none}, at **N=20 for the reference config (`clock=none`×`cache=all`) / N=10 for the five non-reference configs / escalate the reference to N=30** on a pre-registered trigger (modality/outlier flag, boundary-proximity, any out-of-range repeat, discrete-with-≥2-values); repeats collected as R shuffled sweeps, one fresh process per profile.
  - Positive Tests: the accounting identity `collected == classified + excluded_typed` holds and the study reproduces the historical admission set bit-for-bit via the imported `dispersion_gate` (the admission gate is demoted to a `would_have_been_admitted` reporting flag, not a filter); a NAMED uncensored `determinism_study` artifact (distinct from the admitted-only `matrix_summary.json`) classifies every `(cell,metric,config)` into the priority-ordered taxonomy {`out_of_range`/`vendor_defect`, `insufficient_evidence`, `multimodal`, `deterministic`, `quasi_stable`, `clock_noise`, `noisy`, `unstable`} via `sigma_rel` (d₂(N)-normalized for range rules, unit-asserted); pct-overflow rows are retained-typed (`ncu_pct_overflow`, never clamped/imputed) with per-(metric,config) OOR rate reported and the host-locked reference confirmed r=0; the two-stage small-N modality detector flags at tier-1 and certifies only at N=30; accuracy-gate denominators are the pooled P90 floor + per-cell override with the per-class rules; cross-protocol sensitivity is reported on the two SEPARATE axes (clock=instrument / cache=state) by equivalence testing; a cost/resume gate lets the sweep stop and resume without recomputing completed chunks.
  - Negative Tests: any admitted row produced by clipping or normalizing an out-of-range NCU percentage fails; an adaptive stop-when-CV-stable rule used for N (optional-stopping bias) fails; back-to-back same-process (non-shuffled) repeat collection fails the run-order design; a cell dropped without a typed exclusion reason (breaking the accounting identity) fails; a single-support outlier classified as `multimodal` fails; the classification statistic diverging from the imported `dispersion_gate` (a re-introduced scale bug) fails the lockstep property test.

- AC-8: **The full-roster corpus is frozen with a complete, defensible provenance and exclusion ledger.**
  - Positive Tests: the sanctioned widened freezer (DEC-3) emits, per admitted cell, every sm_86-available registered metric (mean + dispersion + repeatability rule), plus trace/control-bit/card-evidence manifests, source/protocol/tool hashes, and the `trace_contract_id`; a single typed-exclusion authority ledger classifies every non-captured cell with a reason ∈ {`arch_gate`, `semantic_mismatch`, `trace_unsupported`, `repeatability_failed`, `correctness_failed`, `calibration_inconclusive`, `host_capacity_deferred`} and satisfies `denominator == universe − Σ(typed exclusions)`; `shape_adjusted` is recorded as capture provenance on runnable cells, never as an exclusion reason; the freezer's `binary_sha256` check uses equality (the substring-idiom bug is fixed); the frozen bundle reports zero unresolved `problems`.
  - Negative Tests: a bundle with a cell in neither admitted outputs nor the ledger fails; a ledger row with a null/undefined `typed_reason` fails; a freezer that admits a cell whose label and trace `binary_sha256` differ (but one contains the other as a substring) fails.
```

```python
"""Targets-not-features static guard (successor to the retired NCU quarantine guard).

CLAUDE.md Rule 1 (re-founded 2026-07-02): NCU / hardware-counter values are
prediction *targets and validation references only*. They must never enter
simulator inputs: no label-artifact reads, no NCU invocation, and no
label-pipeline imports inside ``sim/`` — except the ``sim/emit`` observation-
contract surface, whose whole job is to name the metrics being emitted (metric
*names* are the output contract and are allowed everywhere; label *values* are
not).

Scope now, growth later: scans ``sim/`` sources (Rust + Python + build files).
The eval harness may read labels (it scores predictions against them); the
label factory obviously touches NCU. Those trees are exempt by construction.

Run:  .venv/bin/python -m pytest eval/guards/test_targets_not_features_guard.py -q
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
from pathlib import Path

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
SIM_ROOT = REPO_ROOT / "sim"

# Token classes forbidden inside sim/ sources. Word-ish boundaries where the
# bare token would otherwise be a common substring.
FORBIDDEN_PATTERNS: dict[str, re.Pattern[str]] = {
    "label artifact path": re.compile(r"data/local_labels|data/annex"),
    "admitted-label artifact": re.compile(r"label_admitted|label_repeatability|label_excluded"),
    "NCU report artifact": re.compile(r"\.ncu-rep|ncu_report|ncu-report"),
    "label-pipeline import": re.compile(
        r"(import_label|parse_ncu_metrics|capture_(count|timing|cache)_labels|"
        r"repeatability_aggregator)"
    ),
    "NCU invocation": re.compile(r"""(?x)
        (subprocess|Command::new|process::Command)[^\n]{0,120}
        ["'](/[\w/.-]*)?ncu["' ]
    """),
}

# The emission layer's contract surface may name metrics/contracts freely; it
# still must not read label artifacts, so only the import/invocation classes
# are relaxed there — artifact-path classes stay enforced.
EMIT_RELAXED_CLASSES = {"label-pipeline import"}
SCAN_SUFFIXES = {".rs", ".py", ".toml", ".cc", ".cpp", ".h", ".hpp"}


def _sim_sources():
    if not SIM_ROOT.is_dir():
        return
    # Cargo build-output dirs, excluded EXPLICITLY (never by bare component
    # name: a future source dir that happens to be called "target" must stay
    # scanned).
    excluded_build_dirs = (
        ("target",),                    # sim/target/
        ("trace", "pbgen", "target"),   # sim/trace/pbgen/target/
    )
    for p in sorted(SIM_ROOT.rglob("*")):
        if not (p.is_file() and p.suffix in SCAN_SUFFIXES):
            continue
        rel_parts = p.relative_to(SIM_ROOT).parts
        if any(rel_parts[: len(pre)] == pre for pre in excluded_build_dirs):
            continue
        yield p


def test_sim_sources_never_consume_ncu_labels():
    violations: list[str] = []
    scanned = 0
    for src in _sim_sources():
        scanned += 1
        rel = src.relative_to(REPO_ROOT)
        in_emit = "emit" in src.relative_to(SIM_ROOT).parts[:2]
        text = src.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        for cls, pat in FORBIDDEN_PATTERNS.items():
            if in_emit and cls in EMIT_RELAXED_CLASSES:
                continue
            for m in pat.finditer(text):
                line = text.count("\n", 0, m.start()) + 1
                violations.append(f"{rel}:{line}: [{cls}] {m.group(0)[:80]!r}")
    assert not violations, (
        "targets-not-features violations inside sim/ "
        "(NCU labels are targets, never inputs):\n" + "\n".join(violations)
    )
    # sim/ may legitimately not exist before the Phase-0 scaffold lands; the
    # registry assertion below still runs, so the guard is never vacuous.


def test_metric_registry_declares_model_io_role_or_deployability():
    """Transition assertion for the Rule-1 enforcement field.

    Registry v2 entries carry ``deployability`` today; the Phase-1B migration
    renames it to ``model_io_role`` (target | input | either). Every metric
    must carry exactly one of the two — a metric with neither is unguardable.
    """
    reg_path = REPO_ROOT / "tools/local_labels/metric_registry.json"
    doc = json.loads(reg_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    metrics = doc.get("metrics", doc)
    if isinstance(metrics, dict):
        items = metrics.items()
    else:
        items = ((m.get("label_field", str(i)), m) for i, m in enumerate(metrics))
    missing = [
        name for name, spec in items
        if isinstance(spec, dict)
        and "deployability" not in spec
        and "model_io_role" not in spec
    ]
    assert not missing, (
        "metric_registry entries lacking both 'deployability' and "
        f"'model_io_role': {missing}"
    )
```

```rust
//! sim-calib: loader for the per-arch calibration cards
//! (`arch_profiles/cards/*.json`).
//!
//! The simulator's latencies, geometries, and clock anchors come from these
//! cards — which are filled from microbenchmarks (pchase) and vendor
//! documentation, never from NCU labels (Rule 1: targets-never-features).
//!
//! Fail-closed discipline: a card field recorded as a `research_gap` is a
//! typed error when the simulator requires it — there are no silent default
//! latencies. The schema's `objOrGap` pattern applies at BLOCK level too
//! (the sm_120-laptop card ships `l1_geometry` itself as a research_gap
//! object), so gap-able blocks deserialize through [`Carded`]. An object
//! carrying BOTH a payload and a `status` key is rejected outright rather
//! than silently admitted as either arm. The Python side enforces
//! card↔evidence lockstep (`arch_profiles/tests/test_arch_profile_lockstep.py`);
//! this crate's own lockstep test asserts the Rust loader sees the identical
//! sm_86 values.

use std::path::Path;

use serde::Deserialize;
use serde::de::DeserializeOwned;

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum CalibError {
    #[error("could not read card {path}: {source}")]
    Io {
        path: String,
        #[source]
        source: std::io::Error,
    },
    #[error("could not parse card {path}: {source}")]
    Parse {
        path: String,
        #[source]
        source: serde_json::Error,
    },
    #[error("card {arch_id} has no latencies_cycles block (pre-Phase-1 card?)")]
    LatenciesMissing { arch_id: String },
    #[error(
        "card {arch_id}: latency '{kind}' is a research_gap ({reason}); the simulator must not \
         invent a default — close the gap with a microbenchmark or type-exclude the consumer"
    )]
    ResearchGap {
        arch_id: String,
        kind: LatencyKind,
        reason: String,
    },
    #[error(
        "card {arch_id}: block '{block}' is a research_gap ({reason}); the consumer must \
         type-exclude, never guess geometry"
    )]
    BlockResearchGap {
        arch_id: String,
        block: &'static str,
        reason: String,
    },
    #[error("card field {field} = {value} violates '{requirement}' — fail closed, never clamp")]
    InvalidCardValue {
        field: &'static str,
        value: String,
        requirement: &'static str,
    },
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub enum LatencyKind {
    L1Hit,
    L2Hit,
    Dram,
    Shared,
    MshrOutstandingMisses,
}

impl std::fmt::Display for LatencyKind {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        let s = match self {
            Self::L1Hit => "l1_hit",
            Self::L2Hit => "l2_hit",
            Self::Dram => "dram",
            Self::Shared => "shared",
            Self::MshrOutstandingMisses => "mshr_outstanding_misses",
        };
        f.write_str(s)
    }
}

/// Closed enum so a typo'd status never deserializes into a gap arm.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum GapStatus {
    #[serde(rename = "research_gap")]
    ResearchGap,
}

/// A measured scalar card property: value + dispersion + unit.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct MeasuredValue {
    pub value: f64,
    pub dispersion: Option<f64>,
    pub unit: Option<String>,
}

/// A scalar property that is either measured or a typed research gap.
/// Mirrors the ArchProfile schema's `objOrGap` shape. Deserialization is
/// manual (NOT `#[serde(untagged)]`): an object with BOTH `value` and
/// `status` keys — a half-migrated card — must fail parsing, not silently
/// win whichever untagged arm matches first.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum Measured {
    Value(MeasuredValue),
    Gap { reason: Option<String> },
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
struct RawObjOrGap {
    #[serde(default)]
    value: Option<f64>,
    #[serde(default)]
    dispersion: Option<f64>,
    #[serde(default)]
    unit: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    status: Option<GapStatus>,
    #[serde(default)]
    reason: Option<String>,
}

impl<'de> Deserialize<'de> for Measured {
    fn deserialize<D>(deserializer: D) -> Result<Self, D::Error>
    where
        D: serde::Deserializer<'de>,
    {
        let raw = RawObjOrGap::deserialize(deserializer)?;
        match (raw.value, raw.status) {
            (Some(value), None) => Ok(Measured::Value(MeasuredValue {
                value,
                dispersion: raw.dispersion,
                unit: raw.unit,
            })),
            (None, Some(GapStatus::ResearchGap)) => Ok(Measured::Gap { reason: raw.reason }),
            (Some(_), Some(_)) => Err(serde::de::Error::custom(
                "objOrGap entry carries BOTH `value` and `status: research_gap` — \
                 half-migrated card, refusing to pick an arm",
            )),
            (None, None) => Err(serde::de::Error::custom(
                "objOrGap entry carries NEITHER `value` nor `status: research_gap`",
            )),
        }
    }
}

/// A whole card block (object payload) that may instead be a typed
/// research-gap object — the block-level `objOrGap` pattern used by the
/// partial sm_120-laptop card for `l1_geometry` and
/// `nvml_reference_operating_point`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum Carded<T> {
    Present(T),
    Gap { reason: Option<String> },
}

impl<T> Carded<T> {
    pub fn require(&self, arch_id: &str, block: &'static str) -> Result<&T, CalibError> {
        match self {
            Carded::Present(t) => Ok(t),
            Carded::Gap { reason } => Err(CalibError::BlockResearchGap {
                arch_id: arch_id.to_string(),
                block,
                reason: reason.clone().unwrap_or_default(),
            }),
        }
    }

    /// Serde default for blocks newer than a card: a field the card
    /// never carried is a GAP (fail closed at `require`), never a value.
    /// The reason names BOTH causes — cards ignore unknown keys, so a
    /// misspelled block key lands here too, and the diagnosis must not
    /// claim the card simply predates the block (gate finding
    /// 2026-07-07).
    pub fn absent_gap() -> Self {
        Carded::Gap {
            reason: Some(
                "field absent from this card (card predates the block, OR the card's key is \
                 misspelled — unknown keys are ignored on parse)"
                    .to_owned(),
            ),
        }
    }
}

impl<'de, T> Deserialize<'de> for Carded<T>
where
    T: DeserializeOwned,
{
    fn deserialize<D>(deserializer: D) -> Result<Self, D::Error>
    where
        D: serde::Deserializer<'de>,
    {
        let raw = serde_json::Value::deserialize(deserializer)?;
        let status = raw.get("status");
        if let Some(status) = status {
            let gap: GapStatus = serde_json::from_value(status.clone())
                .map_err(|e| serde::de::Error::custom(format!("bad gap status: {e}")))?;
            let GapStatus::ResearchGap = gap;
            let reason = raw
                .get("reason")
                .and_then(|r| r.as_str())
                .map(str::to_string);
            return Ok(Carded::Gap { reason });
        }
        serde_json::from_value(raw)
            .map(Carded::Present)
            .map_err(|e| serde::de::Error::custom(format!("block payload: {e}")))
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct LatenciesCycles {
    pub l1_hit: Measured,
    pub l2_hit: Measured,
    pub dram: Measured,
    pub shared: Measured,
    pub mshr_outstanding_misses: Measured,
    pub provenance: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct L1Geometry {
    pub l1tex_bytes: u64,
    pub ways: u32,
    pub default_l1_bytes: u64,
    pub max_shared_bytes: u64,
    pub sets: u32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct L2Geometry {
    pub capacity_bytes: u64,
    pub sector_bytes: u32,
    pub num_slices: u32,
    pub line_bytes: u32,
    /// `null` on partial cards (sm_120-laptop ships capacity/slices with the
    /// write policy still unmeasured) — consumers requiring the policy must
    /// treat `None` as a typed gap, never assume write-allocate.
    pub policy: Option<String>,
    pub provenance: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct NvmlReferenceOperatingPoint {
    pub sm_clock_ref_mhz: f64,
    pub max_sm_clock_mhz: f64,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub short_burst_sm_clock_ref_mhz: Option<f64>,
}

/// Aggregate DRAM read-bandwidth covariate at the card-declared model
/// operating point. On the retained sm_86 card this combines the vendor
/// bus-width × data-rate spec with legacy numeric core/memory clock fields;
/// schema-v1 pchase provenance does not prove those clocks were locked during
/// latency capture. Vendor-documented simulator INPUT, never an NCU value;
/// `peak_bytes_per_second` remains a service-rate hypothesis until verified
/// schema-v2 protocol evidence supersedes it.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct DramBandwidth {
    pub peak_bytes_per_second: u64,
    pub locked_sm_clock_mhz: f64,
    pub locked_mem_clock_mhz: f64,
    pub provenance: String,
}

impl DramBandwidth {
    /// The card-declared SM model clock in Hz as an exact integer (the legacy
    /// JSON field is named `locked_sm_clock_mhz`; that name is not proof of the
    /// retained capture protocol). The fixed-point DRAM server divides by this
    /// value. VALIDATED: a non-finite, non-positive
    /// or non-whole-MHz value is a typed error — a float→int saturating
    /// cast of a bad card value would otherwise produce a 0 Hz clock and
    /// a silently inert bandwidth server (gate finding, 2026-07-07).
    pub fn locked_sm_clock_hz(&self) -> Result<u64, CalibError> {
        let mhz = self.locked_sm_clock_mhz;
        if !mhz.is_finite() || mhz <= 0.0 || mhz.fract() != 0.0 {
            return Err(CalibError::InvalidCardValue {
                field: "dram_bandwidth.locked_sm_clock_mhz",
                value: mhz.to_string(),
                requirement: "positive finite whole MHz",
            });
        }
        Ok((mhz as u64) * 1_000_000)
    }

    /// The carded peak rate, validated non-zero (a 0 B/s rate must be a
    /// typed configuration error, never a clamped 1 B/s server).
    pub fn validated_peak_bytes_per_second(&self) -> Result<u64, CalibError> {
        if self.peak_bytes_per_second == 0 {
            return Err(CalibError::InvalidCardValue {
                field: "dram_bandwidth.peak_bytes_per_second",
                value: "0".to_owned(),
                requirement: "positive bytes/second",
            });
        }
        Ok(self.peak_bytes_per_second)
    }
}

/// Typed bundle of the latencies every current consumer requires (MSHR is
/// deliberately NOT here: it is still a research gap on sm_86 and consumers
/// must confront that via [`ArchCard::require_latency`]).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct RequiredLatencies {
    pub l1_hit: f64,
    pub l2_hit: f64,
    pub dram: f64,
    pub shared: f64,
}

/// The subset of an ArchProfile card the simulator consumes. Unknown fields
/// are ignored on purpose: cards carry label-side bookkeeping (predictor
/// state, crosswalk gates) that must never leak into simulator inputs.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct ArchCard {
    pub arch_id: String,
    pub sku: String,
    pub compute_capability: (u32, u32),
    pub sm_count: u32,
    pub l1_geometry: Carded<L1Geometry>,
    pub l2_geometry: Carded<L2Geometry>,
    pub nvml_reference_operating_point: Carded<NvmlReferenceOperatingPoint>,
    /// DRAM read-bandwidth covariate; research_gap on cards without a
    /// locked operating point (sm_120-laptop). Absent on legacy cards →
    /// treated as a gap, never a default.
    #[serde(default = "Carded::absent_gap")]
    pub dram_bandwidth: Carded<DramBandwidth>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub latencies_cycles: Option<LatenciesCycles>,
    /// Load classifier default for bare LDG.E (per-arch measured fact).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub bare_ldg_e_default: Option<String>,
}

impl ArchCard {
    pub fn load(path: &Path) -> Result<Self, CalibError> {
        let raw = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|source| CalibError::Io {
            path: path.display().to_string(),
            source,
        })?;
        serde_json::from_str(&raw).map_err(|source| CalibError::Parse {
            path: path.display().to_string(),
            source,
        })
    }

    /// The latency table, or a typed error if the card predates Phase 1D.
    pub fn latencies(&self) -> Result<&LatenciesCycles, CalibError> {
        self.latencies_cycles
            .as_ref()
            .ok_or_else(|| CalibError::LatenciesMissing {
                arch_id: self.arch_id.clone(),
            })
    }

    /// A required latency in cycles. `research_gap` → typed error, never a
    /// default.
    pub fn require_latency(&self, kind: LatencyKind) -> Result<f64, CalibError> {
        let lat = self.latencies()?;
        let entry = match kind {
            LatencyKind::L1Hit => &lat.l1_hit,
            LatencyKind::L2Hit => &lat.l2_hit,
            LatencyKind::Dram => &lat.dram,
            LatencyKind::Shared => &lat.shared,
            LatencyKind::MshrOutstandingMisses => &lat.mshr_outstanding_misses,
        };
        match entry {
            Measured::Value(v) => Ok(v.value),
            Measured::Gap { reason } => Err(CalibError::ResearchGap {
                arch_id: self.arch_id.clone(),
                kind,
                reason: reason.clone().unwrap_or_default(),
            }),
        }
    }

    /// The four simulator-required latencies resolved at once, so a consumer
    /// can fail-close at construction instead of mid-simulation.
    pub fn required_latencies(&self) -> Result<RequiredLatencies, CalibError> {
        Ok(RequiredLatencies {
            l1_hit: self.require_latency(LatencyKind::L1Hit)?,
            l2_hit: self.require_latency(LatencyKind::L2Hit)?,
            dram: self.require_latency(LatencyKind::Dram)?,
            shared: self.require_latency(LatencyKind::Shared)?,
        })
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
#[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)] // unwrap in tests is an assertion, not error handling
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::path::PathBuf;

    fn card_path(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
        PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))
            .join("../../arch_profiles/cards")
            .join(name)
            .canonicalize()
            .expect("card exists in-repo")
    }

    /// Cross-language lockstep: the Rust loader must see the exact retained
    /// values the Python lockstep test pins against the legacy schema-v1
    /// pchase observation. The v1 card does not establish a locked protocol.
    #[test]
    fn sm86_card_lockstep() {
        let card = ArchCard::load(&card_path("sm_86.json")).expect("load sm_86 card");
        assert_eq!(card.arch_id, "sm_86");
        assert_eq!(card.compute_capability, (8, 6));
        assert_eq!(card.sm_count, 84);
        let l2 = card.l2_geometry.require("sm_86", "l2_geometry").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(l2.capacity_bytes, 6_291_456);
        assert_eq!(l2.num_slices, 6);
        assert_eq!(l2.sector_bytes, 32);
        assert_eq!(l2.line_bytes, 128);
        let l1 = card.l1_geometry.require("sm_86", "l1_geometry").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(l1.l1tex_bytes, 131_072);
        assert_eq!(l1.default_l1_bytes, 32_768);
        assert_eq!(card.bare_ldg_e_default.as_deref(), Some("ca"));

        // Retained legacy pchase-v1 observations. They are scoped to the
        // recorded operating state; successor values require verified v2
        // recapture metadata for the core:memory clock relationship.
        let l = card.required_latencies().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(l.l1_hit, 33.0016);
        assert_eq!(l.l2_hit, 243.6152);
        assert_eq!(l.dram, 422.6486);
        assert_eq!(l.shared, 23.0016);

        // Retained DRAM service-rate hypothesis: vendor 21 Gbps × 384-bit
        // combined with the card's existing 1350/10501 MHz model point. The
        // legacy pchase-v1 card does not prove those clocks were locked during
        // latency capture.
        let bw = card
            .dram_bandwidth
            .require("sm_86", "dram_bandwidth")
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(bw.peak_bytes_per_second, 1_008_000_000_000);
        assert_eq!(bw.locked_sm_clock_mhz, 1350.0);
        assert_eq!(bw.locked_mem_clock_mhz, 10501.0);
        assert_eq!(bw.locked_sm_clock_hz().unwrap(), 1_350_000_000);
        assert_eq!(
            bw.validated_peak_bytes_per_second().unwrap(),
            1_008_000_000_000
        );
        // INTERNAL-CONSISTENCY PIN: the card carries the same numeric SM-clock
        // model point in its NVML and DRAM blocks. Keep those numeric hypotheses
        // aligned so the latency arm and byte server do not silently use
        // different clock domains. Equality is not evidence that the legacy v1
        // pchase capture ran under a locked protocol.
        let nvml = card
            .nvml_reference_operating_point
            .require("sm_86", "nvml_reference_operating_point")
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            bw.locked_sm_clock_mhz, nvml.sm_clock_ref_mhz,
            "dram_bandwidth.locked_sm_clock_mhz must equal the NVML reference SM clock"
        );
        // Bad-value validation is typed, never a clamp/saturating cast.
        let bad = DramBandwidth {
            peak_bytes_per_second: 0,
            locked_sm_clock_mhz: -1.0,
            locked_mem_clock_mhz: 10501.0,
            provenance: String::new(),
        };
        assert!(matches!(
            bad.locked_sm_clock_hz(),
            Err(CalibError::InvalidCardValue { .. })
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            bad.validated_peak_bytes_per_second(),
            Err(CalibError::InvalidCardValue { .. })
        ));

        // Ordering sanity mirrors the Python lockstep test.
        assert!(l.shared < l.l1_hit);
        assert!(l.l1_hit < l.l2_hit);
        assert!(l.l2_hit < l.dram);
    }

    /// The PARTIAL sm_120-laptop card must load: its gap blocks surface as
    /// typed BlockResearchGap on access, never as an opaque parse failure.
    #[test]
    fn sm120_partial_card_loads_with_typed_block_gaps() {
        let card = ArchCard::load(&card_path("sm_120-laptop.json")).expect("load sm_120 card");
        assert_eq!(card.arch_id, "sm_120");
        // l2_geometry is populated on this card.
        let l2 = card.l2_geometry.require("sm_120", "l2_geometry").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(l2.capacity_bytes, 33_554_432);
        // l1_geometry + NVML op point are research gaps → typed errors.
        match card.l1_geometry.require("sm_120", "l1_geometry") {
            Err(CalibError::BlockResearchGap { block, reason, .. }) => {
                assert_eq!(block, "l1_geometry");
                assert!(!reason.is_empty());
            }
            other => panic!("expected typed BlockResearchGap, got {other:?}"),
        }
        assert!(matches!(
            card.nvml_reference_operating_point
                .require("sm_120", "nvml_reference_operating_point"),
            Err(CalibError::BlockResearchGap { .. })
        ));
        // No latency table yet → typed LatenciesMissing.
        assert!(matches!(
            card.require_latency(LatencyKind::L1Hit),
            Err(CalibError::LatenciesMissing { .. })
        ));
        // DRAM bandwidth is a research gap (no locked operating point on
        // the DVFS-spanning laptop part) → typed, never inherited.
        assert!(matches!(
            card.dram_bandwidth.require("sm_120", "dram_bandwidth"),
            Err(CalibError::BlockResearchGap { .. })
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn sm86_mshr_is_research_gap_typed() {
        let card = ArchCard::load(&card_path("sm_86.json")).expect("load sm_86 card");
        match card.require_latency(LatencyKind::MshrOutstandingMisses) {
            Err(CalibError::ResearchGap { kind, reason, .. }) => {
                assert_eq!(kind, LatencyKind::MshrOutstandingMisses);
                assert!(!reason.is_empty(), "gap must carry its reason");
            }
            other => panic!("expected typed ResearchGap, got {other:?}"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_latency_block_is_typed() {
        let card_json = r#"{
            "arch_id": "sm_test", "sku": "test",
            "compute_capability": [8, 6], "sm_count": 1,
            "l1_geometry": {"l1tex_bytes": 1, "ways": 1, "default_l1_bytes": 1,
                             "max_shared_bytes": 1, "sets": 1},
            "l2_geometry": {"capacity_bytes": 1, "sector_bytes": 32, "num_slices": 1,
                             "line_bytes": 128, "policy": "p", "provenance": "test"},
            "nvml_reference_operating_point": {"sm_clock_ref_mhz": 1.0, "max_sm_clock_mhz": 1.0}
        }"#;
        let card: ArchCard = serde_json::from_str(card_json).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(
            card.require_latency(LatencyKind::L1Hit),
            Err(CalibError::LatenciesMissing { .. })
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn typo_gap_status_never_deserializes() {
        let bad = r#"{"status": "reseach_gap", "reason": "typo"}"#;
        assert!(serde_json::from_str::<Measured>(bad).is_err());
    }

    /// The half-migrated collision case: an entry carrying BOTH a value and
    /// a research_gap status must fail parsing outright — under the old
    /// untagged representation it silently deserialized as Value(0.0).
    #[test]
    fn value_and_status_collision_rejected() {
        let bad = r#"{"status": "research_gap", "reason": "pending", "value": 0.0}"#;
        let err = serde_json::from_str::<Measured>(bad).expect_err("collision must fail");
        assert!(err.to_string().contains("BOTH"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn empty_entry_rejected() {
        assert!(serde_json::from_str::<Measured>(r#"{"unit": "cycles"}"#).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn carded_typo_status_rejected() {
        let bad = r#"{"status": "reseach_gap", "reason": "typo"}"#;
        assert!(serde_json::from_str::<Carded<L1Geometry>>(bad).is_err());
    }
}
```
