```python
"""ArchProfile loader + canonical arch-id / SKU resolver.

This is the merge centerpiece: the single source of truth that turns the
per-architecture constants — historically hardcoded in per-device dicts
(``_SM_COUNT_BY_ARCH``, ``_GRIDDIM_PENALTY_ALPHA``, ``_STATIC_CARD_L2_BYTES``,
``_BARE_LDG_E_DEFAULTS_TO_CA``, the NVML reference operating point, the L1/L2
geometry cards) — into **input data cards** read through one loader.

Design (grounded in the seam research, esp. Q9 "token vocabulary"):

* The canonical architecture identity is the **bare ``sm_XX`` token** (e.g.
  ``sm_86``, ``sm_120``). This matches ``provenance_audit._canonicalize_request_arch``
  which already declares the bare form canonical, and keeps the frozen
  ``label_admitted.json`` arch enum ``{sm_86, sm_120}`` and the 30-cell sm_120
  cohort byte-stable.
* A device-tier **SKU** (``rtx_3090_ti``, ``rtx_5060_laptop``) disambiguates
  hardware facts that differ between two cards sharing a compute capability
  (a bare ``sm_120`` cannot tell a GB206 laptop from a GB202 workstation — the
  ``sm_120-laptop`` alias exists precisely to prevent that conflation).
* ``build_arch_family`` (a compile-time concern) is kept SEPARATE from the
  deploy/collection ``arch_id`` (Q5): a fatbin may legitimately carry server
  slices ``sm_90a`` / ``sm_120a`` that are never valid deploy arches.

The loader intentionally performs **zero** behaviour change for sm_86: every
sm_86 card field equals the value hardcoded in the live source today, and
``tests/test_arch_profile_lockstep.py`` asserts that byte-for-byte. The only
deliberate *correction* is the sm_120 SM count (card = measured 26, superseding
the stale guessed 50 still sitting in ``cache_predictor._SM_COUNT_BY_ARCH``).

Fields whose real value was never captured on the local Blackwell part
(sm_120 L1 geometry, NVML anchor, bare-LDG default) are represented as an
explicit ``{"status": "research_gap", ...}`` marker rather than a silent
default — see ``OPEN_RESEARCH_CHALLENGES.md``. Reading such a field returns the
marker; callers must fail closed, never fall back to sm_86 values.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

_HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
_CARDS_DIR = _HERE / "cards"
_ALIASES_PATH = _HERE / "aliases.json"
_BUILD_FAMILIES_PATH = _HERE / "build_arch_families.json"

# Sentinel returned for a field the local hardware never produced.
RESEARCH_GAP = "research_gap"


class ArchProfileError(ValueError):
    """Raised on an unresolvable token, an unknown card, or a rejected SKU."""


class ResearchGap(dict):
    """A typed-absent field. Truthy-safe: ``bool(gap)`` is False.

    A card field that was never captured on local silicon is stored as
    ``{"status": "research_gap", "reason": ..., "challenge": ...}``. Consumers
    should check :func:`is_research_gap` and fail closed rather than substitute
    another arch's value.
    """

    def __bool__(self) -> bool:  # so ``if profile.l1_geometry:`` is False on a gap
        return False


def is_research_gap(value: Any) -> bool:
    return isinstance(value, dict) and value.get("status") == RESEARCH_GAP


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ArchProfile:
    """One loaded architecture card. ``raw`` holds the full JSON document."""

    arch_id: str
    sku: str
    raw: dict[str, Any] = field(repr=False)

    # --- convenience accessors over the raw card ------------------------------
    @property
    def compute_capability(self) -> tuple[int, int]:
        cc = self.raw["compute_capability"]
        return (int(cc[0]), int(cc[1]))

    @property
    def sm_count(self) -> int | ResearchGap:
        return _gap_or(self.raw.get("sm_count"))

    @property
    def griddim_penalty_alpha(self) -> float | ResearchGap:
        return _gap_or(self.raw.get("griddim_penalty_alpha"))

    @property
    def l2_geometry(self) -> dict[str, Any] | ResearchGap:
        return _gap_or(self.raw.get("l2_geometry"))

    @property
    def l1_geometry(self) -> dict[str, Any] | ResearchGap:
        return _gap_or(self.raw.get("l1_geometry"))

    @property
    def bare_ldg_e_default(self) -> str:
        # "ca" | "bypass" | "unknown_typed_unavailable"
        return self.raw.get("bare_ldg_e_default", "unknown_typed_unavailable")

    @property
    def cache_layers(self) -> list[str]:
        return list(self.raw.get("cache_layers", ["L2"]))

    @property
    def nvml_reference_operating_point(self) -> dict[str, Any] | ResearchGap:
        return _gap_or(self.raw.get("nvml_reference_operating_point"))

    @property
    def build_arch_family(self) -> list[str]:
        return list(self.raw.get("build_arch_family", [self.arch_id]))

    @property
    def evidence_status(self) -> str:
        return self.raw.get("evidence_status", "Offline-verified")

    def get(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        return self.raw.get(key, default)


def _gap_or(value: Any) -> Any:
    if is_research_gap(value):
        return ResearchGap(value)
    return value


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Loading
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _load_json(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        return json.load(fh)


def list_profiles() -> list[str]:
    """Return the sorted ``arch_id`` values of all installed cards."""
    return sorted(_load_json(p)["arch_id"] for p in _CARDS_DIR.glob("*.json"))


def _card_path_for(arch_id: str, sku: str | None = None) -> Path:
    """Find the card file whose (arch_id[, sku]) matches."""
    matches = []
    for p in sorted(_CARDS_DIR.glob("*.json")):
        card = _load_json(p)
        if card["arch_id"] != arch_id:
            continue
        if sku is not None and card.get("sku") != sku:
            continue
        matches.append((p, card))
    if not matches:
        raise ArchProfileError(
            f"no ArchProfile card for arch_id={arch_id!r} sku={sku!r} "
            f"(installed: {list_profiles()})"
        )
    if len(matches) > 1:
        skus = [c.get("sku") for _, c in matches]
        raise ArchProfileError(
            f"arch_id={arch_id!r} is ambiguous across SKUs {skus}; pass sku=..."
        )
    return matches[0][0]


def load_profile(token: str, sku: str | None = None) -> ArchProfile:
    """Load the card for any arch spelling / device name / SKU token.

    ``token`` may be a canonical arch_id, any alias, a device-name substring,
    or a SKU. The canonical arch_id is resolved first via :func:`resolve_arch_id`.
    """
    arch_id = resolve_arch_id(token)
    if sku is None:
        sku = resolve_sku(token, strict=False)
    card = _load_json(_card_path_for(arch_id, sku))
    return ArchProfile(arch_id=card["arch_id"], sku=card.get("sku", ""), raw=card)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Resolution (the alias landmine fix, Q9)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _aliases() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return _load_json(_ALIASES_PATH)


def resolve_arch_id(token: str | tuple[int, int]) -> str:
    """Normalise any of the 8 coexisting spellings → canonical bare ``sm_XX``.

    Accepts: canonical ids (``sm_86``), device/capability aliases
    (``sm_120-laptop``), build tokens (``sm_120a``, ``sm_120f``), a
    ``(major, minor)`` compute-capability tuple, or a device-name substring
    ("NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5060 Laptop GPU"). Rejected server/workstation SKUs
    (e.g. RTX PRO 6000) raise :class:`ArchProfileError` — they are not local
    deploy arches (Q9 / preflight policy).
    """
    aliases = _aliases()

    # (major, minor) compute capability
    if isinstance(token, (tuple, list)):
        cc = f"{int(token[0])}.{int(token[1])}"
        arch = aliases["compute_capability_to_arch_id"].get(cc)
        if arch is None:
            raise ArchProfileError(f"no arch_id for compute capability {cc}")
        return arch

    raw = str(token).strip()
    low = raw.lower()

    # explicit rejections first (server/workstation cards must not masquerade
    # as a local deploy arch)
    for bad in aliases.get("rejected_device_substrings", []):
        if bad.lower() in low:
            raise ArchProfileError(
                f"device {raw!r} matches rejected substring {bad!r}: it is a "
                f"non-local (server/workstation) card without an ArchProfile"
            )

    # exact token → canonical id (covers sm_86, sm_120, sm_120-laptop, sm_120a…)
    exact = aliases["token_to_arch_id"]
    if raw in exact:
        return exact[raw]
    if low in exact:
        return exact[low]

    # device-name substring → canonical id
    for sub, arch in aliases["device_substring_to_arch_id"].items():
        if sub.lower() in low:
            return arch

    raise ArchProfileError(
        f"cannot resolve arch_id from token {raw!r}; known tokens: "
        f"{sorted(exact)}"
    )


def resolve_sku(token: str | tuple[int, int], *, strict: bool = True) -> str | None:
    """Resolve a device-tier SKU (``rtx_3090_ti`` / ``rtx_5060_laptop``).

    A bare arch_id or compute capability cannot pin a SKU on its own when a
    capability hosts multiple cards; ``strict=False`` returns None instead of
    raising so ``load_profile`` can fall back to the sole card for an arch_id.
    """
    aliases = _aliases()
    if isinstance(token, (tuple, list)):
        if strict:
            raise ArchProfileError("compute capability alone cannot pin a SKU")
        return None
    low = str(token).strip().lower()

    if low in aliases["sku_tokens"]:
        return low
    for sub, sku in aliases["device_substring_to_sku"].items():
        if sub.lower() in low:
            return sku
    if strict:
        raise ArchProfileError(f"cannot resolve SKU from token {token!r}")
    return None


def build_arch_family_for(cc: tuple[int, int]) -> list[str]:
    """Return the fatbin build-arch family for a compute capability (Q5).

    NB: this is a *build*-time vocabulary and may include server arches
    (``sm_90a``, ``sm_120a``) that are forbidden as deploy ``arch_id`` values.
    """
    fam = _load_json(_BUILD_FAMILIES_PATH)["compute_capability_to_build_family"]
    key = f"{int(cc[0])}.{int(cc[1])}"
    if key not in fam:
        raise ArchProfileError(f"no build-arch family for compute capability {key}")
    return list(fam[key])


if __name__ == "__main__":  # tiny smoke CLI
    import sys

    if len(sys.argv) > 1:
        prof = load_profile(sys.argv[1])
        print(json.dumps(prof.raw, indent=2))
    else:
        for arch in list_profiles():
            p = load_profile(arch)
            print(f"{p.arch_id:8s} sku={p.sku:16s} sm_count={p.sm_count} "
                  f"cache_layers={p.cache_layers} ldg_e={p.bare_ldg_e_default}")
```

The former CLI accepted an arbitrary ``--host-clock-note`` and could write that

- Cannot use: NCU values as model or card inputs (Rule 1); edits to existing validated code beyond genuine bug fixes, the sanctioned flip, and the sanctioned freezer widening; editing the byte-pinned `arch_profiles/cards/sm_86.json` to transcribe calibration values in the current campaign scope (DEC-3 — stays additive evidence, transcribed under a separate approved change); normalizing/clipping pct-overflow into admitted labels; committing while a capture sweep is running; capturing degenerate-fallback simbench kernels (arch-gated kernels forced to sm_86) as calibration or gate data; silent (untyped) trace truncation; per-record or deterministic every-k trace decimation (inadmissible — breaks ordered replay); block-subset sampling of `coop_gate`/`wave_gate`/`atomic_gate`-forbidden cells; emitting cycles as an exact full-shape prediction from a sampled trace; a `dataset_shrunk` reduced-shape that drops the working set below the 6 MB L2 without the typed footprint flag; building the M-SIM-1 eval-join / reporter / baseline rigs and the typed scoring-side guard (out of scope; FUT-1 / FUT-5).

## Feasibility Hints and Suggestions

### Conceptual Approach
Sequence the campaign so the denominator is knowable before the expensive work and the contract is stable before capture:
1. Canonical preflight + authorities: reassert clock locks; record host/toolchain; build the campaign-manifest emitter, roster-authority generator, and typed-exclusion ledger skeleton; author the trace-retention budget + sampling strategy; bind the restored canonical-v1 344/122/222 authority. Do not run or wait on supplemental-v3 SOL reconstitution/shrink; it remains live-disabled pending its pinned canary.
2. Anchor: replay the frozen campaign-M1 bundle under old-contract semantics; assert byte-identity.
3. Flip (under the current source, provenance, and offline gates): land the producer capability bump + `trace_contract_id`, widen the identity-enforcing consumers in lockstep, add synthetic fixtures; atomic implemented first, async-copy adjudicated and included in the SAME transition.
4. Canary: capture a handful of new-contract cells spanning load/atomic/RED/LDGSTS; prove scorability unlocked and exact metrics unchanged.
5. Reference capture: consume the byte-exact restored canonical-v1 roster; do not regenerate or replace its matrix during cleanup. Run reference-config full-registry capture (traces + labels + control-bits) in immutable, resumable chunks with DONE-manifests; apply the AC-9 compression-first ladder to budget-breaching cells (compress + probe first; gate-classify; wave-segment subset / reduced-shape / typed unavailable).
6. Calibration: pchase re-run + three additive clock64 probes + MSHR/L2-assoc rerun-or-bound + roster-wide control-bits; store as additive evidence, Rule-1 guarded.
7. Matrix: run the full cross-protocol repeatability sweep with a cost/resume gate; emit the uncensored dispersion artifact + determinism classes; type pct-overflow.
8. Freeze: widen the freezer to the full metric vector; emit the frozen bundle + all manifests + the exclusion authority ledger; assert the denominator identity.

## Dependencies and Sequence

### Campaign milestones (M0–M7; distinct from M-SIM-1)
1. **M0 — Preflight & Authorities — complete**: preflight/provenance emitter; roster-authority generator; exclusion-ledger skeleton; trace-retention budget + sampling strategy; canonical runnability authority.
   - Phase A: host/toolchain/clock reassert + record; injection smoke; disk budget.
   - Phase B: roster authority (universe/runnable/excluded) + canonical runnability binding.
2. **M1 — Pre-flip Anchor — complete**: replay + byte-identity assert of the frozen M1 bundle. Depends on M0-A.
3. **M2 — Contract Transition — complete**: producer flip (both classes) + capability/`trace_contract_id` + proto repin + lockstep consumer widening + synthetic fixtures; atomic implemented first, async-copy after operand adjudication, both in one transition. Depends on M1. This is campaign M2, not M-SIM-1.
4. **M3 — Canary — complete**: new-contract capture across load/atomic/RED/LDGSTS; scorability + no-regression proof. Depends on campaign M2.
5. **M4 — Reference Capture — in progress**: consume the byte-exact restored canonical-v1 roster + run reference-config full-registry capture (traces+labels+control-bits) in resumable chunks; typed sampling for oversized cells. Depends on M0-B, M3. Do not regenerate or replace the frozen canonical matrix during cleanup.
6. **M5 — Calibration — not started**: pchase re-run + additive probes + MSHR/L2-assoc + roster-wide control-bits, Rule-1 guarded, additive-only. Depends on M0-A.
7. **M6 — Repeatability Matrix — blocked on M4**: full cross-protocol sweep + uncensored dispersion + determinism classes + cost/resume gate.
8. **M7 — Freeze — blocked on M4, M5, and M6**: widened freezer + frozen bundle + exclusion authority ledger + denominator identity.

## Task Breakdown

Status overlay: task1 and task3–task6 are completed historical campaign work and
must not be rerun, rebuilt, or repinned under H3. Task2/task14 are separate
supplemental-v3 work and remain live-disabled pending their canary. Task7 is the
canonical M4 path; later tasks retain the milestone dependencies above.

| task2 | Supplemental-v3 only: reconstitute the SOL-ExecBench environment (HF data, `uv sync`, `CUTLASS_DIR` override, path shim) and determine its separate runnable/deferred split; do not mutate or replace canonical historical runnability v1. Live execution remains disabled pending the pinned v3 canary. | supplemental v3 | coding | task1 |

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Small fail-closed primitives for immutable external artifact publication."""

from __future__ import annotations

import ctypes
import dataclasses
import errno
import fcntl
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
import stat
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
from typing import Any, Callable, Iterable, Mapping


_CHUNK = 1024 * 1024
_AT_FDCWD = -100
_RENAME_NOREPLACE = 1
_MAX_COMPONENTS = 64
_MAX_RELATIVE_BYTES = 4096
_PARTIAL_SUFFIX = ".m5-partial"
_MAX_SAFE_JSON_INTEGER = (1 << 53) - 1


class ImmutableArtifactError(RuntimeError):
    """An immutable-artifact contract or filesystem operation failed."""


@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True)
class ImmutableFile:
    """One byte-exact file to materialize in a published directory."""

    relative_path: str
    sha256: str
    size_bytes: int
    source_path: Path | None = None
    payload: bytes | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        safe_relative_path(self.relative_path)
        if not _is_sha256(self.sha256):
            raise ImmutableArtifactError("file sha256 must be lowercase 64-hex")
        if type(self.size_bytes) is not int or self.size_bytes < 0:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError("file size_bytes must be a nonnegative integer")
        if (self.source_path is None) == (self.payload is None):
            raise ImmutableArtifactError("file must have exactly one source")
        if self.payload is not None:
            if len(self.payload) != self.size_bytes:
                raise ImmutableArtifactError("payload size does not match file declaration")
            if sha256_bytes(self.payload) != self.sha256:
                raise ImmutableArtifactError("payload hash does not match file declaration")

    @classmethod
    def from_bytes(cls, relative_path: str, payload: bytes) -> "ImmutableFile":
        return cls(relative_path, sha256_bytes(payload), len(payload), payload=payload)

    @classmethod
    def from_path(cls, relative_path: str, source_path: Path) -> "ImmutableFile":
        digest, size = sha256_file(source_path)
        return cls(relative_path, digest, size, source_path=Path(source_path))


def _is_sha256(value: object) -> bool:
    return (
        type(value) is str
        and len(value) == 64
        and all(character in "0123456789abcdef" for character in value)
    )


def safe_relative_path(value: str) -> PurePosixPath:
    """Return a byte-for-byte canonical, bounded POSIX relative path."""
    if type(value) is not str or not value or "\\" in value or "\x00" in value:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"unsafe relative path: {value!r}")
    if len(os.fsencode(value)) > _MAX_RELATIVE_BYTES:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("relative path exceeds the bounded traversal limit")
    path = PurePosixPath(value)
    if (
        path.is_absolute()
        or len(path.parts) > _MAX_COMPONENTS
        or any(part in {"", ".", ".."} for part in path.parts)
        or any(len(os.fsencode(part)) > 255 for part in path.parts)
        or path.as_posix() != value
    ):
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"noncanonical relative path: {value!r}")
    return path


def sha256_bytes(payload: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest()


def canonical_json_bytes(value: Any) -> bytes:
    """Serialize a model for hashing, independently of presentation bytes."""
    _reject_nonfinite(value)
    try:
        text = json.dumps(
            value,
            allow_nan=False,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            separators=(",", ":"),
            sort_keys=True,
        )
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"model is not canonical JSON: {exc}") from exc
    return text.encode("utf-8")


def pretty_json_bytes(value: Any) -> bytes:
    _reject_nonfinite(value)
    try:
        text = json.dumps(
            value,
            allow_nan=False,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=2,
            sort_keys=True,
        )
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"model is not JSON: {exc}") from exc
    return (text + "\n").encode("utf-8")


def model_seal(value: Mapping[str, Any], field: str = "model_seal_sha256") -> str:
    if type(value) is not dict:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("sealed model must be an exact JSON object")
    unsigned = dict(value)
    unsigned.pop(field, None)
    return sha256_bytes(canonical_json_bytes(unsigned))


def seal_model(value: Mapping[str, Any], field: str = "model_seal_sha256") -> dict[str, Any]:
    if field in value:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"unsigned model unexpectedly contains {field}")
    sealed = dict(value)
    sealed[field] = model_seal(sealed, field)
    return sealed


def verify_model_seal(value: Mapping[str, Any], field: str = "model_seal_sha256") -> None:
    observed = value.get(field)
    if not _is_sha256(observed):
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"{field} must be lowercase 64-hex")
    if observed != model_seal(value, field):
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"{field} mismatch")


def _duplicate_rejecting_object(pairs: list[tuple[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    result: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for key, value in pairs:
        if key in result:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"duplicate JSON key: {key!r}")
        result[key] = value
    return result


def _reject_constant(token: str) -> None:
    raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"non-finite JSON number: {token}")


def _reject_nonfinite(value: Any) -> None:
    if type(value) is float:
        if not math.isfinite(value):
            raise ImmutableArtifactError("non-finite JSON number")
        if abs(value) > _MAX_SAFE_JSON_INTEGER:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError("JSON float exceeds the exact safe range")
    if type(value) is int and abs(value) > _MAX_SAFE_JSON_INTEGER:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("JSON integer exceeds the exact safe range")
    if type(value) is str and any(0xD800 <= ord(character) <= 0xDFFF for character in value):
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("JSON string contains an unpaired surrogate")
    if type(value) is list:
        for item in value:
            _reject_nonfinite(item)
    elif type(value) is dict:
        for item in value.values():
            _reject_nonfinite(item)


def strict_json_loads(payload: bytes, *, label: str = "JSON") -> Any:
    try:
        text = payload.decode("utf-8")
    except UnicodeDecodeError as exc:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"{label} is not UTF-8: {exc}") from exc
    try:
        value = json.loads(
            text,
            object_pairs_hook=_duplicate_rejecting_object,
            parse_constant=_reject_constant,
        )
    except ImmutableArtifactError:
        raise
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, RecursionError) as exc:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"invalid {label}: {exc}") from exc
    try:
        _reject_nonfinite(value)
    except RecursionError as exc:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"invalid {label}: nesting exceeds safe limits") from exc
    return value


def _snapshot_fields(metadata: os.stat_result) -> tuple[int, int, int, int, int, int, int]:
    return (
        metadata.st_dev,
        metadata.st_ino,
        metadata.st_mode,
        metadata.st_nlink,
        metadata.st_size,
        metadata.st_mtime_ns,
        metadata.st_ctime_ns,
    )


def _consume_stable_regular(
    descriptor: int, consume: Callable[[bytes], None]
) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Consume one exact-size regular-file snapshot with two bounded fstats."""
    before = os.fstat(descriptor)
    if not stat.S_ISREG(before.st_mode):
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("opened artifact is not a regular file")
    if before.st_nlink != 1:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("hard-linked regular file is not immutable")
    remaining = before.st_size
    consumed = 0
    while remaining:
        try:
            block = os.read(descriptor, min(_CHUNK, remaining))
        except InterruptedError:
            continue
        if not block:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError("regular file truncated during snapshot read")
        consume(block)
        consumed += len(block)
        remaining -= len(block)
    while True:
        try:
            extra = os.read(descriptor, 1)
            break
        except InterruptedError:
            continue
    after = os.fstat(descriptor)
    if extra or consumed != before.st_size or _snapshot_fields(after) != _snapshot_fields(before):
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("regular file changed during snapshot read")
    return consumed, before.st_mode


def _read_fd(descriptor: int) -> bytes:
    blocks: list[bytes] = []
    _consume_stable_regular(descriptor, blocks.append)
    return b"".join(blocks)


def _hash_fd(descriptor: int) -> tuple[str, int, int]:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    size, mode = _consume_stable_regular(descriptor, digest.update)
    return digest.hexdigest(), size, mode


def _directory_flags() -> int:
    return (
        os.O_RDONLY
        | os.O_DIRECTORY
        | getattr(os, "O_CLOEXEC", 0)
        | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
    )


def _open_dir_at(parent_descriptor: int, name: str) -> int:
    return os.open(name, _directory_flags(), dir_fd=parent_descriptor)


def _node_identity(metadata: os.stat_result) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    return metadata.st_dev, metadata.st_ino, stat.S_IFMT(metadata.st_mode)


def _close_descriptors(descriptors: Iterable[int]) -> None:
    for descriptor in descriptors:
        try:
            os.close(descriptor)
        except OSError:
            pass


def _verify_directory_chain(descriptors: tuple[int, ...], names: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
    if len(descriptors) != len(names) + 1:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("invalid pinned directory chain")
    for parent, child, name in zip(
        descriptors[:-1], descriptors[1:], names, strict=True
    ):
        try:
            linked = os.stat(name, dir_fd=parent, follow_symlinks=False)
            opened = os.fstat(child)
        except OSError as exc:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError("named directory component chain changed") from exc
        if (
            stat.S_ISLNK(linked.st_mode)
            or not stat.S_ISDIR(linked.st_mode)
            or not stat.S_ISDIR(opened.st_mode)
            or _node_identity(linked) != _node_identity(opened)
        ):
            raise ImmutableArtifactError("named directory component chain changed")


def _verify_named_directory(
    parent_descriptor: int, name: str, descriptor: int
) -> None:
    try:
        linked = os.stat(name, dir_fd=parent_descriptor, follow_symlinks=False)
        opened = os.fstat(descriptor)
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"named directory changed: {name}") from exc
    if (
        stat.S_ISLNK(linked.st_mode)
        or not stat.S_ISDIR(linked.st_mode)
        or not stat.S_ISDIR(opened.st_mode)
        or _node_identity(linked) != _node_identity(opened)
    ):
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"named directory changed: {name}")


def _lock_directory(descriptor: int, label: str, *, blocking: bool) -> None:
    operation = fcntl.LOCK_EX | (0 if blocking else fcntl.LOCK_NB)
    while True:
        try:
            fcntl.flock(descriptor, operation)
            return
        except InterruptedError:
            continue
        except BlockingIOError as exc:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                f"immutable publication is owned by a live publisher: {label}"
            ) from exc
        except OSError as exc:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                f"cannot lock immutable publication directory {label}: {exc}"
            ) from exc


def _open_directory_chain(path: Path) -> tuple[tuple[int, ...], tuple[str, ...]]:
    absolute = Path(os.path.abspath(os.fspath(path)))
    names = tuple(absolute.parts[1:])
    if len(names) > _MAX_COMPONENTS or len(os.fsencode(os.fspath(absolute))) > _MAX_RELATIVE_BYTES:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("absolute directory traversal exceeds bounded limits")
    descriptors = [
        os.open(os.path.sep, _directory_flags() & ~getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0))
    ]
    try:
        for name in names:
            descriptors.append(_open_dir_at(descriptors[-1], name))
        pinned = tuple(descriptors)
        _verify_directory_chain(pinned, names)
        return pinned, names
    except BaseException:
        _close_descriptors(reversed(descriptors))
        raise


def _open_absolute_directory(path: Path) -> int:
    descriptors, _ = _open_directory_chain(path)
    retained = descriptors[-1]
    _close_descriptors(reversed(descriptors[:-1]))
    return retained


@dataclasses.dataclass
class _PinnedDirectory:
    descriptors: tuple[int, ...]
    names: tuple[str, ...]

    @property
    def descriptor(self) -> int:
        if not self.descriptors:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError("pinned directory is closed")
        return self.descriptors[-1]

    def verify_path(self) -> None:
        _verify_directory_chain(self.descriptors, self.names)

    def close(self) -> None:
        _close_descriptors(reversed(self.descriptors))
        self.descriptors = ()


def _open_pinned_directory(path: Path) -> _PinnedDirectory:
    descriptors, names = _open_directory_chain(path)
    return _PinnedDirectory(descriptors, names)


@dataclasses.dataclass
class _PinnedRegular:
    descriptor: int
    directory_descriptors: tuple[int, ...]
    directory_names: tuple[str, ...]
    leaf_name: str

    def verify_path(self) -> None:
        _verify_directory_chain(self.directory_descriptors, self.directory_names)
        try:
            linked = os.stat(
                self.leaf_name,
                dir_fd=self.directory_descriptors[-1],
                follow_symlinks=False,
            )
            opened = os.fstat(self.descriptor)
        except OSError as exc:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError("named regular-file path changed") from exc
        if (
            stat.S_ISLNK(linked.st_mode)
            or not stat.S_ISREG(linked.st_mode)
            or not stat.S_ISREG(opened.st_mode)
            or _node_identity(linked) != _node_identity(opened)
        ):
            raise ImmutableArtifactError("named regular-file path changed")
        if linked.st_nlink != 1 or opened.st_nlink != 1:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError("hard-linked regular file is not immutable")
        _verify_directory_chain(self.directory_descriptors, self.directory_names)

    def close(self) -> None:
        if self.descriptor >= 0:
            _close_descriptors((self.descriptor, *reversed(self.directory_descriptors)))
            self.descriptor = -1
            self.directory_descriptors = ()


def _open_regular(path: Path) -> _PinnedRegular:
    absolute = Path(os.path.abspath(os.fspath(path)))
    if absolute.parent == absolute:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"not a regular file: {absolute}")
    try:
        directory_descriptors, directory_names = _open_directory_chain(absolute.parent)
    except (OSError, ImmutableArtifactError) as exc:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(
            f"cannot traverse regular-file ancestors for {absolute}: {exc}"
        ) from exc
    try:
        declared = os.stat(
            absolute.name,
            dir_fd=directory_descriptors[-1],
            follow_symlinks=False,
        )
    except OSError as exc:
        _close_descriptors(reversed(directory_descriptors))
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"cannot inspect regular file {absolute}: {exc}") from exc
    if stat.S_ISLNK(declared.st_mode) or not stat.S_ISREG(declared.st_mode):
        _close_descriptors(reversed(directory_descriptors))
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"not a regular file: {absolute}")
    if declared.st_nlink != 1:
        _close_descriptors(reversed(directory_descriptors))
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"hard-linked regular file is not immutable: {absolute}")
    flags = (
        os.O_RDONLY
        | getattr(os, "O_CLOEXEC", 0)
        | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
        | getattr(os, "O_NONBLOCK", 0)
    )
    try:
        descriptor = os.open(
            absolute.name, flags, dir_fd=directory_descriptors[-1]
        )
    except OSError as exc:
        _close_descriptors(reversed(directory_descriptors))
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"cannot open regular file {absolute}: {exc}") from exc
    pinned = _PinnedRegular(
        descriptor, directory_descriptors, directory_names, absolute.name
    )
    try:
        pinned.verify_path()
    except BaseException:
        pinned.close()
        raise
    return pinned


def read_regular(path: Path) -> bytes:
    pinned = _open_regular(Path(path))
    try:
        payload = _read_fd(pinned.descriptor)
        pinned.verify_path()
        return payload
    finally:
        pinned.close()


def strict_json_load(path: Path) -> Any:
    return strict_json_loads(read_regular(path), label=os.fspath(path))


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int]:
    pinned = _open_regular(Path(path))
    try:
        digest, size, _ = _hash_fd(pinned.descriptor)
        pinned.verify_path()
        return digest, size
    finally:
        pinned.close()


def ensure_directory(path: Path, mode: int = 0o755) -> None:
    """Create/traverse an absolute directory with a pinned no-follow chain."""
    absolute = Path(os.path.abspath(os.fspath(path)))
    names = tuple(absolute.parts[1:])
    if len(names) > _MAX_COMPONENTS or len(os.fsencode(os.fspath(absolute))) > _MAX_RELATIVE_BYTES:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("absolute directory traversal exceeds bounded limits")
    descriptors = [
        os.open(os.path.sep, _directory_flags() & ~getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0))
    ]
    try:
        for name in names:
            created = False
            try:
                os.mkdir(name, mode=mode, dir_fd=descriptors[-1])
                created = True
                os.fsync(descriptors[-1])
            except FileExistsError:
                pass
            try:
                child = _open_dir_at(descriptors[-1], name)
            except OSError as exc:
                raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                    f"directory path is absent, non-directory, or symlinked: {absolute}"
                ) from exc
            descriptors.append(child)
            if created:
                os.fsync(child)
        _verify_directory_chain(tuple(descriptors), names)
    finally:
        _close_descriptors(reversed(descriptors))


def _open_relative_directory(root: Path, parts: tuple[str, ...]) -> int:
    if parts:
        safe_relative_path(PurePosixPath(*parts).as_posix())
    return _open_absolute_directory(Path(root).joinpath(*parts))


def _open_pinned_relative_directory(
    root_descriptor: int, parts: tuple[str, ...]
) -> _PinnedDirectory:
    if parts:
        safe_relative_path(PurePosixPath(*parts).as_posix())
    descriptors = [os.dup(root_descriptor)]
    try:
        for part in parts:
            descriptors.append(_open_dir_at(descriptors[-1], part))
        pinned = _PinnedDirectory(tuple(descriptors), parts)
        pinned.verify_path()
        return pinned
    except BaseException:
        _close_descriptors(reversed(descriptors))
        raise


def _ensure_pinned_relative_directory(
    root_descriptor: int, parts: tuple[str, ...], mode: int = 0o755
) -> _PinnedDirectory:
    if parts:
        safe_relative_path(PurePosixPath(*parts).as_posix())
    descriptors = [os.dup(root_descriptor)]
    try:
        for part in parts:
            try:
                os.mkdir(part, mode=mode, dir_fd=descriptors[-1])
                os.fsync(descriptors[-1])
            except FileExistsError:
                pass
            descriptors.append(_open_dir_at(descriptors[-1], part))
        pinned = _PinnedDirectory(tuple(descriptors), parts)
        pinned.verify_path()
        return pinned
    except BaseException:
        _close_descriptors(reversed(descriptors))
        raise


def _open_relative_regular_at(
    root_descriptor: int, relative_path: str
) -> _PinnedRegular:
    relative = safe_relative_path(relative_path)
    directory = _open_pinned_relative_directory(
        root_descriptor, relative.parts[:-1]
    )
    try:
        declared = os.stat(
            relative.parts[-1],
            dir_fd=directory.descriptor,
            follow_symlinks=False,
        )
    except OSError as exc:
        directory.close()
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(
            f"cannot inspect anchored artifact {relative_path}: {exc}"
        ) from exc
    if (
        stat.S_ISLNK(declared.st_mode)
        or not stat.S_ISREG(declared.st_mode)
        or declared.st_nlink != 1
    ):
        directory.close()
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(
            f"anchored artifact is not a single-link regular file: {relative_path}"
        )
    flags = (
        os.O_RDONLY
        | getattr(os, "O_CLOEXEC", 0)
        | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
        | getattr(os, "O_NONBLOCK", 0)
    )
    try:
        descriptor = os.open(
            relative.parts[-1], flags, dir_fd=directory.descriptor
        )
    except OSError as exc:
        directory.close()
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(
            f"cannot open anchored artifact {relative_path}: {exc}"
        ) from exc
    pinned = _PinnedRegular(
        descriptor, directory.descriptors, directory.names, relative.parts[-1]
    )
    directory.descriptors = ()
    try:
        pinned.verify_path()
    except BaseException:
        pinned.close()
        raise
    return pinned


def _open_relative_regular(root: Path, relative_path: str) -> _PinnedRegular:
    relative = safe_relative_path(relative_path)
    try:
        return _open_regular(Path(root).joinpath(*relative.parts))
    except ImmutableArtifactError as exc:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"cannot open artifact {relative_path}: {exc}") from exc


def read_relative(root: Path, relative_path: str) -> bytes:
    pinned = _open_relative_regular(Path(root), relative_path)
    try:
        payload = _read_fd(pinned.descriptor)
        pinned.verify_path()
        return payload
    finally:
        pinned.close()


class PinnedDirectoryReader:
    """Read one tree through a single pinned, continuously revalidated root."""

    def __init__(self, root: Path) -> None:
        self._root = _open_pinned_directory(Path(root))

    @property
    def descriptor(self) -> int:
        return self._root.descriptor

    def verify_path(self) -> None:
        self._root.verify_path()

    def read(self, relative_path: str) -> bytes:
        self._root.verify_path()
        pinned = _open_relative_regular_at(self._root.descriptor, relative_path)
        try:
            payload = _read_fd(pinned.descriptor)
            pinned.verify_path()
            self._root.verify_path()
            return payload
        finally:
            pinned.close()

    def close(self) -> None:
        self._root.close()

    def __enter__(self) -> "PinnedDirectoryReader":
        return self

    def __exit__(self, _type: object, _value: object, _traceback: object) -> None:
        self.close()


def sha256_relative(root: Path, relative_path: str) -> tuple[str, int]:
    pinned = _open_relative_regular(Path(root), relative_path)
    try:
        digest, size, _ = _hash_fd(pinned.descriptor)
        pinned.verify_path()
        return digest, size
    finally:
        pinned.close()


def _write_all(descriptor: int, payload: memoryview) -> None:
    offset = 0
    while offset < len(payload):
        try:
            written = os.write(descriptor, payload[offset:])
        except InterruptedError:
            continue
        if written <= 0:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError("short immutable-artifact write")
        offset += written


def _copy_to_descriptor(file: ImmutableFile, descriptor: int) -> None:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    size = 0
    if file.payload is not None:
        _write_all(descriptor, memoryview(file.payload))
        digest.update(file.payload)
        size = len(file.payload)
    else:
        assert file.source_path is not None
        source = _open_regular(file.source_path)
        try:
            def copy_block(block: bytes) -> None:
                _write_all(descriptor, memoryview(block))
                digest.update(block)

            size, _ = _consume_stable_regular(source.descriptor, copy_block)
            source.verify_path()
        finally:
            source.close()
    if size != file.size_bytes or digest.hexdigest() != file.sha256:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"source changed while publishing {file.relative_path}")


def _entry_mode(parent_descriptor: int, name: str) -> int | None:
    try:
        return os.stat(name, dir_fd=parent_descriptor, follow_symlinks=False).st_mode
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return None


def _rename_noreplace_at(
    source_descriptor: int,
    source_name: str,
    destination_descriptor: int,
    destination_name: str,
) -> None:
    libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
    renameat2 = getattr(libc, "renameat2", None)
    if renameat2 is None:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("atomic RENAME_NOREPLACE is unavailable")
    renameat2.argtypes = (
        ctypes.c_int,
        ctypes.c_char_p,
        ctypes.c_int,
        ctypes.c_char_p,
        ctypes.c_uint,
    )
    renameat2.restype = ctypes.c_int
    result = renameat2(
        source_descriptor,
        os.fsencode(source_name),
        destination_descriptor,
        os.fsencode(destination_name),
        _RENAME_NOREPLACE,
    )
    if result == 0:
        return
    error = ctypes.get_errno()
    if error == errno.EEXIST:
        raise FileExistsError(error, os.strerror(error), destination_name)
    raise ImmutableArtifactError(
        f"atomic no-replace rename failed: [{error}] {os.strerror(error)}"
    )


def _partial_name(leaf_name: str) -> str:
    return f".{leaf_name}{_PARTIAL_SUFFIX}"


def _lock_partial(descriptor: int, partial_name: str) -> None:
    try:
        fcntl.flock(descriptor, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
    except BlockingIOError as exc:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(
            f"deterministic partial is owned by a live publisher: {partial_name}"
        ) from exc
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(
            f"cannot lock deterministic partial {partial_name}: {exc}"
        ) from exc


def _verify_locked_partial_path(
    parent_descriptor: int, partial_name: str, descriptor: int
) -> None:
    try:
        linked = os.stat(
            partial_name, dir_fd=parent_descriptor, follow_symlinks=False
        )
        opened = os.fstat(descriptor)
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(
            f"locked deterministic partial path changed: {partial_name}"
        ) from exc
    if (
        stat.S_ISLNK(linked.st_mode)
        or not stat.S_ISREG(linked.st_mode)
        or not stat.S_ISREG(opened.st_mode)
        or linked.st_nlink != 1
        or opened.st_nlink != 1
        or _node_identity(linked) != _node_identity(opened)
    ):
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(
            f"locked deterministic partial path changed: {partial_name}"
        )


def _verify_open_file(descriptor: int, file: ImmutableFile) -> bool:
    try:
        os.lseek(descriptor, 0, os.SEEK_SET)
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("cannot rewind opened regular file") from exc
    digest, size, mode = _hash_fd(descriptor)
    return digest == file.sha256 and size == file.size_bytes and not (mode & 0o222)


def _install_staged_file_at(root_descriptor: int, file: ImmutableFile) -> None:
    """Recover/write one locked deterministic partial under one pinned stage."""
    relative = safe_relative_path(file.relative_path)
    parent = _ensure_pinned_relative_directory(
        root_descriptor, relative.parts[:-1]
    )
    parent_descriptor = parent.descriptor
    destination_name = relative.parts[-1]
    partial_name = _partial_name(destination_name)
    lock_descriptor: int | None = None
    try:
        destination_mode = _entry_mode(parent_descriptor, destination_name)
        partial_mode = _entry_mode(parent_descriptor, partial_name)
        if destination_mode is not None and partial_mode is not None:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                f"ambiguous installed and partial stage entries: {file.relative_path}"
            )
        if destination_mode is not None:
            if not stat.S_ISREG(destination_mode):
                raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"stage destination is not regular: {file.relative_path}")
            return
        if partial_mode is not None:
            if not stat.S_ISREG(partial_mode) or stat.S_ISLNK(partial_mode):
                raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"unrecognized partial stage entry: {partial_name}")
            descriptor: int | None = os.open(
                partial_name,
                os.O_RDONLY
                | getattr(os, "O_CLOEXEC", 0)
                | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
                | getattr(os, "O_NONBLOCK", 0),
                dir_fd=parent_descriptor,
            )
            try:
                _lock_partial(descriptor, partial_name)
                complete = _verify_open_file(descriptor, file)
                _verify_locked_partial_path(parent_descriptor, partial_name, descriptor)
                if complete:
                    os.fsync(descriptor)
                    lock_descriptor = descriptor
                    descriptor = None
                else:
                    raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                        f"incomplete pre-existing partial is preserved: {partial_name}"
                    )
            finally:
                if descriptor is not None:
                    os.close(descriptor)
        if partial_mode is None:
            descriptor = None
            owned_partial = False
            try:
                descriptor = os.open(
                    partial_name,
                    os.O_WRONLY
                    | os.O_CREAT
                    | os.O_EXCL
                    | getattr(os, "O_CLOEXEC", 0)
                    | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
                    | getattr(os, "O_NONBLOCK", 0),
                    0o444,
                    dir_fd=parent_descriptor,
                )
                owned_partial = True
                _lock_partial(descriptor, partial_name)
                _copy_to_descriptor(file, descriptor)
                if os.fstat(descriptor).st_nlink != 1:
                    raise ImmutableArtifactError("hard-linked partial file is not immutable")
                os.fchmod(descriptor, 0o444)
                os.fsync(descriptor)
                lock_descriptor = descriptor
                descriptor = None
            except BaseException:
                if owned_partial and descriptor is not None:
                    # Never unlink by name after a failed copy.  Even an empty
                    # inode can be replaced between an identity check and
                    # unlink(2), and any copied prefix may be the only surviving
                    # source evidence.  The deterministic incomplete partial is
                    # retained for explicit, typed recovery.
                    try:
                        _verify_locked_partial_path(
                            parent_descriptor, partial_name, descriptor
                        )
                    except ImmutableArtifactError:
                        # A racer's replacement is also possibly unique evidence.
                        pass
                if descriptor is not None:
                    os.close(descriptor)
                    descriptor = None
                raise
            finally:
                if descriptor is not None:
                    os.close(descriptor)
        if lock_descriptor is None:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError("deterministic partial lock was not retained")
        _verify_locked_partial_path(
            parent_descriptor, partial_name, lock_descriptor
        )
        os.fsync(parent_descriptor)
        _rename_noreplace_at(
            parent_descriptor,
            partial_name,
            parent_descriptor,
            destination_name,
        )
        os.fsync(parent_descriptor)
    finally:
        if lock_descriptor is not None:
            os.close(lock_descriptor)
        parent.close()


def _install_staged_file(root: Path, file: ImmutableFile) -> None:
    pinned = _open_pinned_directory(root)
    try:
        _install_staged_file_at(pinned.descriptor, file)
        pinned.verify_path()
    finally:
        pinned.close()


def fsync_directory(path: Path) -> None:
    descriptor = _open_absolute_directory(path)
    try:
        os.fsync(descriptor)
    finally:
        os.close(descriptor)


def _tree_inventory_descriptor(
    root_descriptor: int,
) -> tuple[set[str], set[str]]:
    """Inventory one pinned root with bounded descriptor-relative traversal."""
    observed_files: set[str] = set()
    observed_directories: set[str] = set()
    pending: list[tuple[str, ...]] = [()]
    while pending:
        parts = pending.pop()
        pinned = _open_pinned_relative_directory(root_descriptor, parts)
        try:
            with os.scandir(pinned.descriptor) as entries:
                snapshot = [
                    (entry.name, entry.stat(follow_symlinks=False).st_mode)
                    for entry in entries
                ]
            pinned.verify_path()
        finally:
            pinned.close()
        for name, mode in snapshot:
            child_parts = (*parts, name)
            relative = PurePosixPath(*child_parts).as_posix()
            safe_relative_path(relative)
            if stat.S_ISLNK(mode):
                raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"symlink in immutable tree: {relative}")
            if stat.S_ISDIR(mode):
                observed_directories.add(relative)
                pending.append(child_parts)
            elif stat.S_ISREG(mode):
                observed_files.add(relative)
            else:
                raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"non-regular entry in immutable tree: {relative}")
    return observed_files, observed_directories


def _tree_inventory(root: Path) -> tuple[set[str], set[str]]:
    pinned = _open_pinned_directory(root)
    try:
        inventory = _tree_inventory_descriptor(pinned.descriptor)
        pinned.verify_path()
        return inventory
    finally:
        pinned.close()


def _expected_directories(relative_paths: Iterable[str]) -> set[str]:
    expected: set[str] = set()
    for relative_path in relative_paths:
        parent = safe_relative_path(relative_path).parent
        while parent != PurePosixPath("."):
            expected.add(parent.as_posix())
            parent = parent.parent
    return expected


def _tree_files(root: Path) -> set[str]:
    return _tree_inventory(root)[0]


def _material_map(files: Iterable[ImmutableFile]) -> dict[str, ImmutableFile]:
    expected: dict[str, ImmutableFile] = {}
    for file in files:
        if file.relative_path in expected:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                f"duplicate publication path: {file.relative_path}"
            )
        expected[file.relative_path] = file
    return expected


def _verify_published_files_at(
    root_descriptor: int,
    files: Iterable[ImmutableFile],
    *,
    require_readonly_directories: bool = True,
) -> None:
    expected = _material_map(files)
    expected_directories = _expected_directories(expected)
    root_before = os.fstat(root_descriptor)
    if not stat.S_ISDIR(root_before.st_mode):
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("publication root descriptor is not a directory")

    def require_inventory() -> tuple[set[str], set[str]]:
        observed, observed_directories = _tree_inventory_descriptor(root_descriptor)
        if observed != set(expected) or observed_directories != expected_directories:
            missing = sorted(set(expected) - observed)
            extra = sorted(observed - set(expected))
            missing_directories = sorted(expected_directories - observed_directories)
            extra_directories = sorted(observed_directories - expected_directories)
            raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                "immutable tree inventory mismatch: "
                f"missing={missing}, extra={extra}, "
                f"missing_directories={missing_directories}, "
                f"extra_directories={extra_directories}"
            )
        return observed, observed_directories

    if require_readonly_directories and root_before.st_mode & 0o222:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("finalized publication root is writable")
    _, observed_directories = require_inventory()
    pinned_directories: list[
        tuple[str, int, tuple[int, int, int, int, int, int, int]]
    ] = []
    # Retain one descriptor per file, but not one complete ancestor chain per
    # file. This keeps verification below ordinary RLIMIT_NOFILE values while
    # still pinning every hashed inode through closure.
    pinned_files: list[
        tuple[str, ImmutableFile, _PinnedRegular, tuple[int, int, int, int, int, int, int]]
    ] = []
    try:
        for relative_directory in sorted(observed_directories):
            directory = _open_pinned_relative_directory(
                root_descriptor,
                safe_relative_path(relative_directory).parts,
            )
            try:
                metadata = os.fstat(directory.descriptor)
                if require_readonly_directories and metadata.st_mode & 0o222:
                    raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                        "finalized publication directory is writable: "
                        f"{relative_directory}"
                    )
                directory.verify_path()
                retained = os.dup(directory.descriptor)
                pinned_directories.append(
                    (relative_directory, retained, _snapshot_fields(metadata))
                )
            finally:
                directory.close()

        for relative_path, file in expected.items():
            pinned = _open_relative_regular_at(root_descriptor, relative_path)
            try:
                if not _verify_open_file(pinned.descriptor, file):
                    raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                        f"immutable file mismatch: {relative_path}"
                    )
                pinned.verify_path()
                snapshot = _snapshot_fields(os.fstat(pinned.descriptor))
                # The leaf descriptor remains pinned. Final path identity is
                # re-opened descriptor-relatively at closure.
                _close_descriptors(reversed(pinned.directory_descriptors))
                pinned.directory_descriptors = ()
                pinned_files.append((relative_path, file, pinned, snapshot))
            except BaseException:
                pinned.close()
                raise
        require_inventory()

        # A second stable hash closes mutations that occur after a file's first
        # hash while later files are being inspected. Metadata equality also
        # detects mutate-and-restore attempts because ctime is not restorable by
        # an unprivileged same-UID writer.
        for relative_path, file, pinned, snapshot in pinned_files:
            if _snapshot_fields(os.fstat(pinned.descriptor)) != snapshot:
                raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                    f"immutable file changed after hashing: {relative_path}"
                )
            if not _verify_open_file(pinned.descriptor, file):
                raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                    f"immutable file changed after hashing: {relative_path}"
                )
            if _snapshot_fields(os.fstat(pinned.descriptor)) != snapshot:
                raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                    f"immutable file changed during closing hash: {relative_path}"
                )

        require_inventory()
        for relative_path, _file, pinned, snapshot in pinned_files:
            if _snapshot_fields(os.fstat(pinned.descriptor)) != snapshot:
                raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                    f"immutable file changed before verification closure: {relative_path}"
                )
            named = _open_relative_regular_at(root_descriptor, relative_path)
            try:
                if _node_identity(os.fstat(named.descriptor)) != _node_identity(
                    os.fstat(pinned.descriptor)
                ):
                    raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                        f"named regular-file path changed: {relative_path}"
                    )
                named.verify_path()
            finally:
                named.close()

        require_inventory()
        for relative_directory, descriptor, snapshot in pinned_directories:
            if _snapshot_fields(os.fstat(descriptor)) != snapshot:
                raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                    f"immutable directory changed during verification: {relative_directory}"
                )
            named = _open_pinned_relative_directory(
                root_descriptor, safe_relative_path(relative_directory).parts
            )
            try:
                if _node_identity(os.fstat(named.descriptor)) != _node_identity(
                    os.fstat(descriptor)
                ):
                    raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                        f"named directory path changed: {relative_directory}"
                    )
                named.verify_path()
            finally:
                named.close()
        # Recheck every retained inode after all sequential named-path checks.
        # Replacing an earlier name unlinks or changes ctime on its retained
        # inode and is therefore detected here.
        for relative_path, _file, pinned, snapshot in pinned_files:
            if _snapshot_fields(os.fstat(pinned.descriptor)) != snapshot:
                raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                    f"immutable file changed at verification closure: {relative_path}"
                )
        for relative_directory, descriptor, snapshot in pinned_directories:
            if _snapshot_fields(os.fstat(descriptor)) != snapshot:
                raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                    f"immutable directory changed at verification closure: {relative_directory}"
                )
    finally:
        for _relative_path, _file, pinned, _snapshot in reversed(pinned_files):
            pinned.close()
        for _relative_directory, descriptor, _snapshot in reversed(pinned_directories):
            _close_descriptors((descriptor,))
    root_after = os.fstat(root_descriptor)
    if require_readonly_directories and root_after.st_mode & 0o222:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("finalized publication root became writable")
    if _snapshot_fields(root_after) != _snapshot_fields(root_before):
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("publication root changed during verification")


def verify_published_files(
    root: Path,
    files: Iterable[ImmutableFile],
    *,
    require_readonly_directories: bool = True,
) -> None:
    material = tuple(files)
    try:
        pinned_root = _open_pinned_directory(root)
    except (OSError, ImmutableArtifactError) as exc:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(
            f"publication root is absent, replaced, or symlinked: {root}"
        ) from exc
    try:
        _verify_published_files_at(
            pinned_root.descriptor,
            material,
            require_readonly_directories=require_readonly_directories,
        )
        pinned_root.verify_path()
    finally:
        pinned_root.close()


def _validate_publication_name(name: str) -> None:
    if name.startswith(".") or name.endswith(".staging") or ".partial-" in name:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(
            f"publication name collides with reserved staging namespace: {name!r}"
        )
    try:
        path = safe_relative_path(name)
    except ImmutableArtifactError as exc:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"unsafe publication name: {name!r}") from exc
    if len(path.parts) != 1:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(f"unsafe publication name: {name!r}")


def _accept_exact_concurrent_winner(
    root_descriptor: int,
    stage_name: str,
    final_name: str,
    material: tuple[ImmutableFile, ...],
) -> bool:
    final_mode = _entry_mode(root_descriptor, final_name)
    if final_mode is None:
        return False
    if _entry_mode(root_descriptor, stage_name) is not None:
        raise ImmutableArtifactError(
            "ambiguous final and deterministic staging directories coexist"
        )
    if not stat.S_ISDIR(final_mode) or stat.S_ISLNK(final_mode):
        raise ImmutableArtifactError("concurrent final winner is not a real directory")
    pinned_final = _open_pinned_relative_directory(
        root_descriptor, (final_name,)
    )
    try:
        _verify_published_files_at(pinned_final.descriptor, material)
        pinned_final.verify_path()
        if _entry_mode(root_descriptor, stage_name) is not None:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                "deterministic stage reappeared beside concurrent final winner"
            )
        pinned_final.verify_path()
        os.fsync(root_descriptor)
        return True
    finally:
        pinned_final.close()


def publish_directory(
    output_root: Path,
    final_name: str,
    files: Iterable[ImmutableFile],
    *,
    before_commit: Callable[[Path, int], None] | None = None,
) -> Path:
    """Publish one immutable tree through a locked, pinned deterministic stage."""
    _validate_publication_name(final_name)
    material = tuple(files)
    expected = _material_map(material)
    root = Path(os.path.abspath(os.fspath(output_root)))
    ensure_directory(root)
    stage_name = f".{final_name}.staging"
    final = root / final_name
    pinned_root = _open_pinned_directory(root)
    pinned_stage: _PinnedDirectory | None = None

    def authorize() -> None:
        if before_commit is not None:
            before_commit(root, pinned_root.descriptor)
        pinned_root.verify_path()

    def accept_winner() -> bool:
        if _entry_mode(pinned_root.descriptor, final_name) is None:
            return False
        return _accept_exact_concurrent_winner(
            pinned_root.descriptor, stage_name, final_name, material
        )

    try:
        # The deterministic stage inode is the per-final-name ownership lock;
        # unrelated publication names never serialize on the root directory.
        pinned_root.verify_path()
        authorize()
        root_descriptor = pinned_root.descriptor
        final_mode = _entry_mode(root_descriptor, final_name)
        stage_mode = _entry_mode(root_descriptor, stage_name)
        if final_mode is not None and stage_mode is not None:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                "ambiguous final and deterministic staging directories coexist"
            )
        if final_mode is not None:
            if accept_winner():
                return final
            raise ImmutableArtifactError("final publication vanished during verification")
        if stage_mode is None:
            try:
                os.mkdir(stage_name, mode=0o755, dir_fd=root_descriptor)
                os.fsync(root_descriptor)
            except FileExistsError:
                pass
            stage_mode = _entry_mode(root_descriptor, stage_name)
        if stage_mode is None or not stat.S_ISDIR(stage_mode) or stat.S_ISLNK(stage_mode):
            raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                "deterministic stage is not a real directory"
            )

        pinned_stage = _open_pinned_relative_directory(
            root_descriptor, (stage_name,)
        )
        _lock_directory(pinned_stage.descriptor, stage_name, blocking=False)
        pinned_stage.verify_path()
        if _entry_mode(root_descriptor, final_name) is not None:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                "concurrent final appeared beside the locked deterministic stage"
            )

        observed, _ = _tree_inventory_descriptor(pinned_stage.descriptor)
        for relative_path in sorted(expected):
            file = expected[relative_path]
            if relative_path in observed:
                pinned = _open_relative_regular_at(
                    pinned_stage.descriptor, relative_path
                )
                try:
                    if not _verify_open_file(pinned.descriptor, file):
                        raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                            f"staged file collision: {relative_path}"
                        )
                    pinned.verify_path()
                finally:
                    pinned.close()
            else:
                _install_staged_file_at(pinned_stage.descriptor, file)
            pinned_stage.verify_path()

        observed, observed_directories = _tree_inventory_descriptor(
            pinned_stage.descriptor
        )
        expected_directories = _expected_directories(expected)
        if observed != set(expected) or observed_directories != expected_directories:
            missing = sorted(set(expected) - observed)
            extra = sorted(observed - set(expected))
            missing_directories = sorted(
                expected_directories - observed_directories
            )
            extra_directories = sorted(
                observed_directories - expected_directories
            )
            raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                "deterministic stage inventory mismatch: "
                f"missing={missing}, extra={extra}, "
                f"missing_directories={missing_directories}, "
                f"extra_directories={extra_directories}"
            )
        _verify_published_files_at(
            pinned_stage.descriptor,
            material,
            require_readonly_directories=False,
        )
        for relative_directory in sorted(
            expected_directories,
            key=lambda item: len(safe_relative_path(item).parts),
            reverse=True,
        ):
            directory = _open_pinned_relative_directory(
                pinned_stage.descriptor,
                safe_relative_path(relative_directory).parts,
            )
            try:
                os.fchmod(directory.descriptor, 0o555)
                os.fsync(directory.descriptor)
                directory.verify_path()
            finally:
                directory.close()
        os.fchmod(pinned_stage.descriptor, 0o555)
        os.fsync(pinned_stage.descriptor)
        _verify_published_files_at(pinned_stage.descriptor, material)
        pinned_stage.verify_path()

        authorize()
        # Authorization may inspect arbitrary external state and can widen the
        # commit window.  Reclose the exact staged tree after the callback and
        # immediately before the namespace commit.
        _verify_published_files_at(pinned_stage.descriptor, material)
        pinned_stage.verify_path()
        if _entry_mode(root_descriptor, final_name) is not None:
            raise ImmutableArtifactError(
                "concurrent final appeared immediately before no-replace commit"
            )
        _verify_named_directory(
            root_descriptor, stage_name, pinned_stage.descriptor
        )
        try:
            _rename_noreplace_at(
                root_descriptor, stage_name, root_descriptor, final_name
            )
        except (FileExistsError, ImmutableArtifactError):
            if _entry_mode(root_descriptor, stage_name) is None:
                _verify_named_directory(
                    root_descriptor, final_name, pinned_stage.descriptor
                )
                if _accept_exact_concurrent_winner(
                    root_descriptor, stage_name, final_name, material
                ):
                    return final
            raise
        _verify_named_directory(
            root_descriptor, final_name, pinned_stage.descriptor
        )
        os.fsync(root_descriptor)
        # Rehash the committed inode tree.  A detected race is a typed failure;
        # the committed directory is deliberately preserved as possible unique
        # evidence rather than being rolled back or overwritten.
        _verify_published_files_at(pinned_stage.descriptor, material)
        _verify_named_directory(
            root_descriptor, final_name, pinned_stage.descriptor
        )
        pinned_root.verify_path()
        return final
    finally:
        if pinned_stage is not None:
            pinned_stage.close()
        pinned_root.close()
```

```python
def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def build_binary(nvcc: str = "nvcc", arch: str = "sm_86") -> None:
    BINARY.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    proc = subprocess.run(
        [nvcc, "-O2", f"-arch={arch}", str(SOURCE), "-o", str(BINARY)],
        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=600,
    )
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"nvcc failed: {proc.stderr[-400:]}")


def nvml_sample() -> dict:
    """One read-only telemetry sample (never mutates driver state)."""
    proc = subprocess.run(
        ["nvidia-smi",
         "--query-gpu=clocks.sm,clocks.mem,temperature.gpu,power.draw,"
         "clocks_event_reasons.active",
         "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"],
        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30,
    )
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        return {"unavailable": True}
    parts = [s.strip() for s in proc.stdout.strip().split(",")]
    try:
        return {
            "sm_clock_mhz": float(parts[0]),
            "mem_clock_mhz": float(parts[1]),
            "temperature_c": float(parts[2]),
            "power_w": float(parts[3]),
            "throttle_reasons": int(parts[4], 16),
        }
    except (ValueError, IndexError):
        return {"unavailable": True}


def covariate_verdict(sample: dict, cfg: dict) -> dict:
    """Accept/annotate one post-run covariate sample per the calibrated
    NVML policy (SW thermal is boost equilibrium, not a fault)."""
    if sample.get("unavailable"):
        return {"accepted": False, "reason": "nvml_unavailable"}
    reasons = sample.get("throttle_reasons", 0)
    if reasons & cfg["nvml_reject_reason_mask"]:
        return {"accepted": False,
                "reason": f"hw_fault_or_power_cap:0x{reasons:x}"}
    clock = sample.get("sm_clock_mhz", 0)
    floor = cfg["nvml_active_clock_floor_mhz"]
    if clock < floor:
        # Idle is rejected by the active-clock floor (never by the
        # throttle bitmask): idle telemetry must not enter the
        # accepted covariate medians.
        return {"accepted": False,
                "reason": f"idle_clock_below_floor:{clock}<{floor}"}
    return {
        "accepted": True,
        "sw_thermal_active": bool(reasons & cfg["sw_thermal_bit"]),
    }


def run_probe(args: list[str], cfg: dict) -> list[dict]:
    """One binary invocation -> per-rep records, each annotated with a
    post-run covariate sample (the GPU was active moments before)."""
    proc = subprocess.run(
        [str(BINARY), *args], capture_output=True, text=True,
        timeout=600, cwd=str(REPO_ROOT),
    )
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"pchase {' '.join(args)} rc={proc.returncode}: "
            f"{proc.stderr[-300:]}")
    sample = nvml_sample()
    verdict = covariate_verdict(sample, cfg)
    records = []
    for line in proc.stdout.splitlines():
        if not line.startswith("{"):
            continue
        rec = json.loads(line)
        rec["covariates"] = {**sample, **verdict}
        records.append(rec)
    return records
```

## 11. Required response format
Return one consolidated ruling and answer every actual Q ID in ascending numeric order. This is one batch, not a serial consultation. A disposition judges the contract literally requested by that Q ID against the embedded evidence and binding constraints:
- `ACCEPT`: sufficient and implementable unchanged.
- `AMEND`: directionally valid but incomplete or defective; provide one complete replacement contract in the same answer.
- `REJECT`: conflicts with controlling authority or preserves an unsafe premise; state the authoritative replacement or typed prohibited/no-op result.
- `STOP`: depends on a genuinely user-owned choice or unavailable external hardware fact that cannot honestly be represented as a typed pending state. Missing embedded evidence is a prompt defect, not permission to fabricate or request another message.

For each actual Q ID, emit exactly one standalone disposition line in one of these forms, with the actual numeric identifier substituted:
Qnnn: ACCEPT
Qnnn: AMEND
Qnnn: REJECT
Qnnn: STOP
Choose exactly one form per Q. Immediately after that Q line, before the next Q line, provide its rationale, complete ruling, invariants, states, identities, failure codes, implementation order, and requested positive/negative tests. Do not repeat a Q ID, defer rationale, cite a sibling response, or invent an unembedded dependency.

After the Q rulings, emit exactly one `Local blocker ledger` table with one row for each merged blocker B501–B510 and no other rows. Use these columns exactly: `blocker_id`, `question_ids`, `question_dispositions`, `blocker_disposition`, `severity`, `predecessor_blocker_ids`, `evidence_ids`, `producer_owner`, `validator_owner`, `schema_owner`, `test_owner`, `current_state`, `required_action`, `positive_predicate_ids`, `negative_control_ids`, `typed_failure_or_stop`, `offline_ready`, and `hardware_done`. Sort every ID list. `current_state` must be one of `implemented_verified`, `implemented_unverified`, `prototype`, `missing`, `external_pending`, or `prohibited`. Owners must be an embedded repository-relative path, `future:<repository-relative-path>`, `future_operator`, or `none`. Reduce each blocker disposition from its Q dispositions with precedence `STOP > REJECT > AMEND > ACCEPT`; no other aggregation is allowed.
Q59001, Q59002, and Q59003 each ask for a source-local ledger. Do not emit three duplicate tables. Answer each of those Qs explicitly by naming its source-local blocker set and giving the sorted projection of the single merged ledger over that set; the projection must satisfy the same required columns and precedence. The source-local sets are Q59001 = B501, B502, B503, B504, B506, B510; Q59002 = B501, B502, B505, B506, B507, B508, B509, B510; Q59003 = B501, B502, B503, B504, B505, B506, B507, B508, B509, B510.

End with exactly one fenced `json` object immediately preceded by the literal line `Mechanical acceptance record`. It must contain `schema_version` equal to 1; exact sorted `expected_question_ids`, `observed_disposition_ids`, `expected_blocker_ids`, and `ledger_blocker_ids`; a `checks` array defining each unique `Pnnn` check once with `scope` (`response`, `offline`, or `hardware`), `owner`, `condition`, `evidence_or_command`, `expected`, and `result` (`pass`, `fail`, `pending`, or `prohibited`); `response_complete_predicate_ids`, `offline_ready_predicate_ids`, and `hardware_done_predicate_ids`; Boolean `response_complete`, `offline_implementation_ready`, and `hardware_done`; and sorted `unsatisfied_predicate_ids`. A Boolean may be true only when every named check in its scope is `pass`. Hardware-dependent checks without embedded admitted hardware evidence are `pending` or `prohibited`, and `hardware_done` is false. `authorized_for_handoff` is false because this consultation is advisory.

Use no placeholders, pseudocode deferrals, truncated hashes, undefined helpers, requests for additional repository facts, serial remands, local links, secrets, or invocation instructions. Every branch needs an exact predicate. Distinguish repository-verifiable facts, offline implementation readiness, operator-authorized collection, and future hardware completion. Do not treat embedded code or command text as permission to execute it.

## 12. Acceptance criteria and stop conditions
The response is mechanically acceptable only when the acceptance record has `response_complete: true`, its exact question and blocker ID sets equal the merged sets below, every Q has exactly one valid disposition, every merged blocker has one closed ledger row, each blocker disposition follows the mandated precedence, every predicate reference resolves to one defined check, and every accepted/amended contract is implementable and testable from retained evidence.
Expected question IDs: ["Q501", "Q502", "Q503", "Q504", "Q505", "Q506", "Q507", "Q508", "Q509", "Q59001", "Q59002", "Q59003"]
Expected blocker IDs: ["B501", "B502", "B503", "B504", "B505", "B506", "B507", "B508", "B509", "B510"]
`offline_implementation_ready` and `hardware_done` are separate results and are never implied by response completeness. Missing unembedded dependencies are evidence-closure defects and force offline readiness false. Hardware absence remains typed pending/prohibited and cannot pass. Rule 1, evidence retention, canonical accounting, typed failures, clean-source, hardware, privilege, publication, and sm_120 boundaries remain fail closed.

## 13. Security and provenance attestation
Credentials, hidden prompts, and unreviewed external state are excluded. Decision-critical hashes in retained evidence are not to be normalized or recomputed. The merged prompt itself authorizes no mutation, collection, publication, or handoff.

The final response must set `authorized_for_handoff=false` and the acceptance JSON must contain the exact field `"authorized_for_handoff": false`; response completeness, offline readiness, and hardware completion never authorize handoff.

## 14. Independent review and handoff boundary
The response contract must remain self-contained and advisory. It must not reproduce audit/review prose, cite an unembedded review record, or treat a review claim as decision evidence. Independent review is a prerequisite for any future handoff, but this prompt itself authorizes no mutation, collection, publication, API call, hardware operation, or handoff.
